Conclusion: 仅需一晚发作间期睡眠数据即可实现高精度致痫区识别，有潜力减少侵入性sEEG监测时长并改善手术结局。

Abstract: Accurate identification of the epileptogenic zone (EZ) is essential for seizure freedom after resective surgery in drug-resistant epilepsy, yet seizure freedom rates remain below 50%. We developed EpiiSLM, a dual foundation model system for EZ identification with stereo-electroencephalography (sEEG), by training a signal foundation model on 104,990 minutes of sEEG recordings from the Montreal Neurological Institute & Hospital, while leveraging all recordings regardless of surgical outcome and anchoring EZ biomarker extraction on non-epileptic signals. A language foundation model then integrates sEEG-derived outputs with multimodal clinical information to produce interpretable predictions. Under leave-one-patient-out evaluation, EpiiSLM achieved 0.978 contact-level positive predictive value (PPV), outperforming the seizure onset zone(SOZ)-as-EZ baseline by 15.1% (p < 0.05), and 100% region-level accuracy; on an external dataset, EpiiSLM achieved 0.857 contact-level PPV. EpiiSLM requires only one night of interictal sleep data, suggesting potential to reduce invasive sEEG monitoring duration and improve surgical outcomes.

</details>


### [58] [GRADE: Graph Representation of LLM Agent Dependency and Execution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22741)
*Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRADE提出将任意LLM智能体的运行过程建模为一种包含执行边（execution edges）和依赖边（dependency edges）的双层图结构，其中依赖边通过已知、观察、声明或推断四种方式评分，能够在运行规模失效时预测失败，并在跨语料迁移中保持优于随机水平的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有trace记录了每一步做了什么，但从未记录每一步依赖了什么、读取了何种状态、重用了哪些结果，缺少对运行间依赖关系的显式建模，导致难以进行有效的失败诊断和优化。

Method: GRADE将任何LLM智能体的运行过程建模为一个图：节点为步骤，边分为两层——执行边（execution edges，从trace中直接读取的运行顺序）和依赖边（dependency edges，每一步依赖的数据或状态，通过已知、观察、声明、推断四种方式分级评分）。

Result: 在涵盖工具使用、编程和网页操作的六个语料库上，依赖层能在运行规模（run size）失效时预测失败，且在留一语料库迁移（leave-one-corpus-out）设置下，所有保留类别上表现均优于随机水平，而运行规模失效；执行层能定位多智能体运行中的故障步骤。

Conclusion: GRADE的统一图表示不仅支持单个运行的失败诊断，还可扩展至大规模运行的效率优化和鲁棒性优化。

Abstract: Can one graph represent every kind of LLM agent's run? A trace records what each step did, never what it relied on, the state it read, and the results it reused. GRADE recovers that missing layer: it models any run as one graph over its step nodes with two edge layers, execution edges (what ran in what order) read from the trace for free, and dependency edges (what each step relied on) rarely logged, so each is graded by how it is known, observed, declared, or inferred. One representation, and each layer earns its place. Across six corpora of LLM agents spanning tool use, coding, and the web, the dependency layer can predict failure where run size is weak and, under leave-one-corpus-out transfer, stays above chance on every held-out class while run size fails. Meanwhile, the execution layer localizes the faulting step in a failed multi-agent run. This work also provides a more in-depth analysis of why generic graph neural networks may misread the dependency layer, unlike our feature-based alternative. The same graph representation opens further uses, carrying from failure diagnosis in a single run to efficiency and robustness optimization at scale.

</details>


### [59] [Noise is Signal: Density-Based Outliers as Leading Indicators of Occupational Emergence in Labor Market Text](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22769)
*Shreyash Rawat*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 论文提出Emergence-Density Inversion (EDI)假说，认为密度聚类中标记为噪声的低密度职位实际上可能代表新兴职业。基于84,988条招聘数据验证，高新兴职业评分(EOS)的离群点组在1.4±0.6季度后转为稳定聚类，并改进EOS指标，预测F1从0.61提升至0.74。回顾性验证和人工评估支持该方法，并发现了Prompt Engineer等四个O*NET中不存在的新兴职业。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准NLP流程将密度聚类中的10-15%噪声职位丢弃，但作者认为在快速演化领域，低发布密度恰恰代表新颖性而非无意义噪声，纠正这一错误认知是核心动机。

Method: 提出EDI假说，使用纵向数据(84,988条招聘，8个季度)验证；扩展EOS指标加入Temporal Velocity和Cross-Platform Convergence；与Isolation Forest、LOF、GLOSH、BERTrend进行对比；进行回顾性验证和人工评估。

Result: 高EOS离群组聚类形成时间为1.4±0.6季度(低EOS为4.1±1.2)，p<0.001；约19%失败；改进后预测F1从0.61提升至0.74；回顾性验证确认提前2-3季度发出信号；人工评估精度77%，kappa=0.74。

Conclusion: 密度聚类中的噪声点可有效用于预测新兴职业，EOS指标经过增强后能可靠地提前2-3季度识别尚未被O*NET收录的职业角色。

Abstract: Standard NLP pipelines for occupational clustering discard the 10-15% of job postings that density-based methods assign to noise. We argue this is an error: in rapidly evolving domains, low posting density signals novelty, not incoherence. We formalize this as the Emergence-Density Inversion (EDI) hypothesis and test it longitudinally on 84,988 job postings across eight quarters (Q4 2022-Q3 2024). EDI is partially confirmed: high-EOS outlier groups transition to stable clusters in 1.4 +/- 0.6 quarters vs. 4.1 +/- 1.2 for low-EOS groups (p < 0.001), though the signal fails in approximately 19% of cases, which we characterize as a failure analysis. We extend the Emerging Occupation Score (EOS) with Temporal Velocity and Cross-Platform Convergence, improving 2-quarter cluster-formation prediction from F1 = 0.61 to 0.74, outperforming Isolation Forest, LOF, GLOSH, and BERTrend baselines. A retrospective study on three now-established roles (MLOps Engineer, DevOps/SRE, Data Engineer) confirms EOS signalled 2-3 quarters before cluster formation, providing held-out validation. A held-out annotator panel (kappa = 0.74) rates EOS > 0.75 as coherent emerging occupations with 77% precision. Prompt Engineer, AI Safety Researcher, Foundation Model Engineer, and Agent Systems Engineer, all absent from O*NET, are top-4 in Q3 2024 and form stable clusters by Q1 2025.

</details>


### [60] [Explainable AI for Mental Health Prediction in Drug-Affected Populations with Dragonfly Algorithm and GAN Oversampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22780)
*Ahnaf Atef Choudhury,Shahriar Siddique Ayon,Md. Ebrahim Hossain,Abdullah Al Mamun*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合PCA-IG特征选择、GAN过采样和蜻蜓算法优化XGBoost的可解释机器学习框架，用于药物使用者的多类心理健康预测，在准确率（94.17%）和加权F1分数（93.80%）上优于传统模型，并通过SHAP提供了实例级可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究忽视了AI在药物使用者心理健康分析中的应用，且存在类别不平衡处理质量低、可解释性差和超参数优化不足等问题，导致预测质量和临床实用性下降。

Method: 采用混合PCA-信息增益（PCA-IG）特征选择降低高维分类数据维度；使用生成对抗网络（GAN）进行过采样以解决类别不平衡；利用蜻蜓算法（DA）优化XGBoost的超参数，构建多类心理健康预测模型；结合SHAP提供实例级可解释性。

Result: DA优化的XGBoost结合GAN过采样达到94.17%准确率和93.80%加权F1分数，优于传统模型；特征分析显示睡眠质量、身体健康和情绪调节是强预测因子，而人口统计学因素影响较小。

Conclusion: 该框架有望生成有效的心理健康预测工具，促进早期干预并改善药物使用者的治疗，且SHAP可解释性增强了临床应用的信任度。

Abstract: Mental illnesses among drug users are an increasing international issue, particularly in regions where early detection cannot be easily undertaken. The current literature tends to ignore the use of AI-based mental health analysis in drug users, and low quality of the class imbalance treatment, low interpretability, and optimal hyperparameter optimization can lower predictive quality and clinical utility. This study present a detailed, explainable machine learning (ML) model of multiclass mental health prediction, using a multidimensional data set of drug-affected persons. We combine hybrid PCA-Information Gain (PCA-IG) feature selection, Generative Adversarial Network (GAN)-based oversampling, and Dragonfly Algorithm (DA)-optimized XGBoost to address some of the limitations of existing methods. The suggested framework is effective to work with high-dimensional categorical data, address the issue of class imbalance, and improve predictive performance due to intelligent hyperparameter tuning. The experimental findings show that the XGBoost model optimized using the DA, in combination with GAN-based oversampling, has an accuracy of 94.17% and a weighted F1-score of 93.80%, which is better than the traditional and baseline models. The behavioral, lifestyle, and health factors, particularly sleep quality, physical health, and emotional regulation, are strongly predictive of mental health, with demographic factors having little impact, as seen through feature analysis. SHAP-based explainable AI provides easy-to-understand, instance-level information, enhancing interpretability and trust in models to be used in clinical settings. The results indicate that this framework has the potential to generate valid mental health forecasting tools, which would facilitate early intervention and enhance the treatment of drug-influenced people.

</details>


### [61] [RLM-Cascade: Response-Level Speculative Decoding for Cost-Efficient LLM API Serving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22840)
*Haifeng Wu,Srinivasan Manoharan,Fangbo Tu,Junhua Zhao,Jian Wan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: RLM-Cascade 是一个代理层系统，通过响应级别的推测解码来降低 LLM API 成本。在 Claude Code 的真实编程工作负载中，该系统实现了 88.8% 的草稿使用率，API 成本降低 45.8%，中位延迟从 3,698ms 降至 2,026ms（1.83 倍加速），同时在基准测试中达到 100% 的通过率（对比基线 95%）。系统开源并已在生产环境部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 降低使用大语言模型 API 的高昂成本，同时不牺牲推理质量和延迟表现，且无需访问模型架构或共享词表。

Method: 提出 RLM-Cascade 代理层系统，包含三个核心组件：(1) 快速廉价的草稿模型生成候选响应；(2) 轻量级复杂度路由器决定是否直接输出草稿（SKIPPED 路径）或交由验证模型处理；(3) 能力强的验证模型接受或增强草稿响应。此外还设计了混合工具调用策略，对模式关键的工具选择轮次绕过推测流水线。

Result: 在 Claude Code 的 125 个生产请求中，草稿使用率达 88.8%；相比直接使用 Opus 基线，API 成本降低 45.8%；中位响应时间从 3,698ms 降至 2,026ms，实现了 1.83 倍加速；在 20 个任务的代码/数学/指令基准测试中达到 100% 通过率（基线为 95%）。

Conclusion: RLM-Cascade 证明了在代理层通过响应级推测解码可以同时实现成本降低、延迟加速和质量提升，打破了通常的成本-速度-质量三角权衡关系。该系统已在企业生产环境中部署并开源。

Abstract: We present RLM-Cascade, a proxy-layer system that applies speculative decoding at the response level to reduce LLM API costs without requiring model architecture access or a shared vocabulary. A fast, inexpensive draft model generates a candidate response; a capable verify model accepts, enhances, or is bypassed entirely depending on a lightweight complexity router. On a real-world agentic coding workload (Claude Code), RLM-Cascade achieves a draft-use rate of 88.8% across 125 production requests, reducing API cost by 45.8% relative to a direct Opus baseline. Counter-intuitively, the proxy also reduces end-to-end latency: median response time is 2,026 ms versus 3,698 ms for Native Opus -- a 1.83X speedup at p50 -- because the SKIPPED path (DeepSeek only, no Opus call) dominates the workload distribution. Quality matches or exceeds the Opus baseline: 100% pass rate on a 20-task Code/Math/Instruct benchmark versus 95% for Native Opus. We further describe a rule-based complexity router that selects the SKIPPED path for simple agentic turns and a hybrid tool-call strategy that bypasses the speculative pipeline for schema-critical tool-selection turns. RLM-Cascade is deployed in production as an enterprise AI infrastructure component and published as open source with a live metrics dashboard and Prometheus endpoint.

</details>


### [62] [Discovering Crystal Structure Prediction Algorithms with an AI Co-Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22866)
*Kiyoung Seong,Nayoung Kim,Sungsoo Ahn*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出 Human-AI Co-discovery (HACO) 系统，通过跨域搜索和稀疏人工引导实现科学算法发现。HACO 从化学组成生成晶体结构的目标出发，跨领域搜索生成建模方法，发现视觉领域的 MaskGIT 适用于晶体结构预测（CSP），并实例化为离散 token 模型 MaskGXT，添加晶体学对称性 token、空间群分层采样和子网格坐标优化。在 MP-20 多态性划分上达到 79.06% METRe 准确率，优于最强基线 70.87%，并在标准 CSP 基准上取得最佳匹配率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索一种跨领域搜索与稀疏人工引导相结合的 AI 协同发现范式，实现科学算法（尤其是晶体结构预测）的自动化发现与优化。

Method: 提出 HACO 系统，跨领域搜索生成建模方法，识别 MaskGIT 并实例化为 MaskGXT；加入晶体学对称性 token、空间群分层采样和子网格坐标细化等模块。

Result: 在 MP-20 多态性划分上 METRe 准确率 79.06%，优于最强基线 70.87%；在标准 MP-20 和 MPTS-52 CSP 基准上取得最佳匹配率。

Conclusion: 在验证成本低廉、快速且对齐良好的领域中，迁移引导的交互式 AI 协作者可通过识别可迁移的建模原则并结合针对性的人工领域指导，有效促进科学算法发现。

Abstract: We introduce Human-AI Co-discovery system (HACO) for scientific algorithm discovery through cross-domain search and sparse human steering. Starting from the goal of generating crystal structures from chemical compositions, HACO searched across generative modeling methodologies from multiple fields and identified MaskGIT, a masked generative model from vision, as a promising framework for crystal structure prediction (CSP). HACO instantiated this masked formulation as a discrete token model of crystal structure; guided by sparse high-level human objectives, it then added crystallographic symmetry tokens, space group stratified sampling for polymorph coverage, and sub-bin coordinate refinement, yielding the Masked Generative Crystal Transformer (MaskGXT). On the MP-20 polymorph split, MaskGXT reaches 79.06% match-everyone-to-reference (METRe) accuracy, compared with 70.87% for the strongest evaluated baseline. MaskGXT also attains the best match rate on standard MP-20 and MPTS-52 CSP benchmarks. These results provide evidence that, in domains offering cheap, fast, and well-aligned validation, transfer-guided interactive AI co-scientists can contribute to scientific algorithm discovery by identifying transferable modeling principles and combining them with targeted human domain guidance.

</details>


### [63] [Physiology-Aware CNN and Zero-Shot Multimodal LLMs for ECG Image Classification: A Comparative Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22889)
*Khalil Ahammad,Derek Abbott,Mohsen Dorraki*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文研究了多模态大语言模型（LLM）和基于CNN的模型在12导联心电图（ECG）图像正常-异常分类任务中的表现。结果显示，CNN模型（尤其是提出的LeadGroupECG）在内部（ROC-AUC 0.92-0.94）和外部数据集（ROC-AUC 0.85-0.86）上表现稳定，而零样本多模态LLM的分类能力接近随机（ROC-AUC约0.5）。尽管LLM能生成合理的ECG描述，但其诊断判别能力有限，领域特定的CNN架构仍是ECG解读的关键。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多模态大语言模型被越来越多地用于解读12导联ECG图像，但其解读往往缺乏验证。ECG图像理解依赖精确波形形态、导联关系和准确的间期测量，与一般图像理解有本质差异。因此需要系统评估LLM在ECG分类任务中的实际能力，并与临床可靠的CNN模型进行对比。

Method: 将标准12导联ECG记录渲染为单页图像，构建二分类（正常vs异常）任务。测试三种LLM（GPT-5.2、GPT-4.1、Gemini-2.5 Pro）使用固定零样本提示词。同时开发了生理感知的CNN模型（LeadGroupECG），能够从预定义的解剖导联组聚合特征，并与ResNet18、DenseNet121、VGG16基线模型比较。在内部测试集和外部PTB-XL数据集上评估。

Result: CNN模型在不同随机种子下表现稳定，内部ROC-AUC为0.92-0.94，外部ROC-AUC为0.85-0.86。提出的LeadGroupECG模型在内部显著优于其骨干网络，且不牺牲外部泛化能力，与其他基线模型竞争力相当，并能一致突出解剖导联组的贡献。相比之下，零样本LLM的分类能力接近随机（ROC-AUC约0.5）。网格校准背景相比无网格背景PR-AUC略有提升。

Conclusion: 尽管多模态LLM能生成合理的ECG描述文本，但其零样本诊断判别能力非常有限。基于临床框架的领域特定架构（如CNN模型）对于基于AI的ECG解读仍然至关重要。

Abstract: Multimodal large language models (LLMs) are increasingly adopted to interpret 12-lead ECG images, though the interpretations often lack validation. However, ECG image understanding significantly differs from general images as it depends on precise waveform morphology, lead relationships and accurate interval measurements. This study investigated whether zero-shot multimodal LLMs can reliably distinguish normal and abnormal ECG images and, in parallel, evaluated CNN-based models for clinically grounded references. Standard 12-lead ECG recordings were rendered as single-page images for a binary normal-abnormal classification task. Three prominent LLMs (GPT-5.2, GPT-4.1, and Gemini-2.5 Pro) were tested using a fixed zero-shot prompt across multiple runs. In parallel, a physiology-aware CNN-based model was developed with the capability to aggregate features from the predefined anatomical lead groups. The model was compared with ResNet18, DenseNet121, VGG16 baselines, and all the models were evaluated on an internal test set and external PTB-XL dataset. Across seeds, CNN-based models demonstrated stable discrimination, with average internal ROC-AUC of 0.92-0.94, and external ROC-AUC of 0.85-0.86. The proposed LeadGroupECG model significantly improved over its backbone internally without compromising external generalization. It remained competitive with other baselines, while consistently highlighting anatomical lead-group contributions. In contrast, zero-shot LLM discrimination remained near-chance (ROC-AUC around 0.5). The PR-AUC improved slightly when ECGs used a grid-based calibration background compared with the grid-free ECGs. Although multimodal LLMs can generate reasonable ECG narratives, their zero-shot diagnostic discrimination remains limited. Therefore, clinically framed, domain-specific architectures remain essential for AI-based ECG interpretation.

</details>


### [64] [Hierarchical Reinforcement Learning for Sparse-Reward Search in Commutative Algebra](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22922)
*Giorgi Butbaia,Paul Orland,Coco Huang,Davide Passaro,Lucas Fagan,Michele Tarquini,Hailong Dao,David Eisenbud,Ali Shehper,Sergei Gukov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于约束选项的层次强化学习框架，结合等变图神经网络策略，用于解决Kalai代数Hirsch猜想的反例构造问题。该框架在稀疏奖励环境下显著优于传统强化学习算法和贪心搜索，首次将层次强化学习应用于交换代数领域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器学习技术应用于长期存在的数学猜想面临极端奖励稀疏的挑战，本文以Kalai代数Hirsch猜想为例，旨在通过强化学习方法构造其反例。

Method: 将反例构造问题转化为图上的稀疏奖励强化学习问题，提出一种基于约束选项的层次强化学习框架，并采用等变图神经网络策略来学习有用的时间抽象。

Result: 在广泛的度数范围内，该方法一致优于传统强化学习算法和贪心搜索。

Conclusion: 通过利用问题的层次结构，首次将层次强化学习成功应用于交换代数领域的问题。

Abstract: Applying machine learning techniques to solving long-standing mathematical conjectures can be particularly challenging due to their extreme reward sparsity. As an illustrative example, we consider Kalai's algebraic Hirsch conjecture and recast the construction of its counterexamples as a sparse-reward reinforcement learning problem on graphs. We propose a constrained options-based HRL framework with an equivariant graph neural network policy, which allows us to learn useful temporal abstractions for this task. We evaluate our approach over a wide range of degrees and demonstrate that it consistently outperforms classical RL algorithms as well as greedy search. By exploiting the hierarchical structure of the problem, we effectively provide a first-of-its-kind application of HRL to a problem in commutative algebra.

</details>


### [65] [EEG Benchmarking Needs a Task Specification Layer: NeuroDoc for Rulebook-Guided, Executable Benchmark Construction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22925)
*Chengxuan Qin,Zhige Chen,Shu Peng,Rui Yang,Jiping Cui,Yikai Dong,Jun Li,Liu Peng,Zhida Shang,Mingze Tang,Kay Chen Tan,Jibin Wu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化任务规范语言和共享规则手册，用于将异构公共EEG数据集标准化为可复用的基准单元，并发布了包含53个条目、245个任务定义的EEG基准语料库，同时开发了NeuroDoc和NeuroAudit工具支持规则手册引导的文档管理，通过四个EEG基础模型验证了该基础设施的可复用性和可执行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 公共EEG数据集缺乏共享的任务规范层，现有标准仅组织文件、元数据和来源，但未以通用语言和规则手册定义EEG任务，导致关键任务语义分散在论文、代码和人工解释中，阻碍了多数据集训练和评估的可复用性。

Method: 将每个基准条目表示为与可执行任务内核同步的任务文档，规则手册定义任务字段、证据要求、文档-内核对齐、审核状态和机器可检查约束；发布包含53个已完成和审核条目、245个任务定义的社区审核EEG基准语料库；开发NeuroDoc和NeuroAudit作为规则手册引导的起草、升级、审核、修订和发布管理的操作支持层。

Result: 成功发布了包含53个条目、245个任务定义的社区审核EEG基准语料库；通过四个EEG基础模型后端的共享下游设置验证了基准单元的可实例化性，提供了基于执行的证据，证明该基础设施是可复用、可审计且可执行的。

Conclusion: 所提出的结构化任务规范语言和共享规则手册能有效将异构EEG数据集标准化为可复用基准单元，NeuroDoc和NeuroAudit为基准管理提供了操作支持，跨模型验证证明了该EEG基准测试基础设施的实用性和可靠性。

Abstract: Electroencephalography (EEG) foundation models increasingly rely on multi-dataset training and evaluation, yet public EEG datasets still lack a shared task specification layer that can turn heterogeneous recordings into reusable benchmark units. Existing standards organize files, metadata, and provenance, but they do not specify EEG tasks under a common language and rulebook, leaving critical task semantics scattered across papers, code, and manual interpretation. We investigate whether heterogeneous public EEG datasets can be standardized through a structured task specification language paired with a shared rulebook. Our methodology represents each benchmark entry as a task document synchronized with an executable task kernel, with the rulebook defining task fields, evidence requirements, document-kernel alignment, review states, and machine-checkable constraints. Using this methodology, we release a community-reviewed EEG benchmark corpus centered on 53 completed and reviewed entries with 245 task definitions spanning diverse paradigms, and we introduce NeuroDoc and NeuroAudit as the operational support layer for rulebook-guided drafting, upgrading, review, amendment, and release management. We further examine whether the resulting benchmark units can be instantiated in a shared downstream setting across four EEG foundation model backbones, providing execution-based evidence for reusable, auditable, and executable EEG benchmarking infrastructure.

</details>


### [66] [FORGE: Fused On-Register Gradient Elimination for Memory-Efficient LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22932)
*Dikshant Kukreja,Kritarth Prasad,Avinash Anand,Zhengkui Wang,Erik Cambria,Timothy Liu,Aik Beng Ng,Simon See,Bapi Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FORGE将优化器步骤融合到反向传播中，以逐块方式在寄存器中即时计算并消费梯度，消除了传统训练中梯度张量的内存占用，同时保持计算等价性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反向模式求导先计算所有梯度并写入内存，优化器再读取，这种两阶段调度导致所有层梯度同时存活，成为训练的内存瓶颈。论文认为这种物化梯度只是微分分阶段的产物，并非学习所需，因此试图消除它。

Method: FORGE将优化器步骤折叠进反向传播中，逐块（tile）在寄存器中完成梯度计算和优化器更新，每个梯度块产生即被消费，从不形成完整张量。融合仅改变更新时机而不改变计算内容：全精度下证明等价；bf16/8-bit下有界偏差且通过随机舍入无偏化；适用于任何逐元素更新规则的线性层，兼容张量/序列并行。

Result: FORGE将优化器步骤的内存占用减半以上，在小批量场景下速度提升约1.5倍；集成到张量并行的Megatron-LM中，可在相同GPU上以标准优化器4倍的微批大小训练8B模型。

Conclusion: 梯度物化是分阶段产物而非学习必需，通过反向传播与优化器的寄存器级融合可以彻底消除梯度张量，显著降低内存并提升速度，且与精度、并行策略和优化器无关。

Abstract: Reverse-mode differentiation computes every weight gradient, writes it to memory, and only then lets the optimizer read it back. This two-phase schedule sets the memory ceiling of modern training: at the seam between the phases, every layer's gradient is live at once. We argue that this materialized gradient is an artifact of how differentiation is staged, not a quantity that learning requires -- and we eliminate it. FORGE folds the optimizer step into the backward pass and applies it one tile at a time, entirely in registers, so each gradient tile is consumed the instant it is produced and never becomes a tensor. The fusion changes only when the update happens, not what it computes: in full precision the fused step is provably exact -- the identical optimizer update, for every element-wise rule -- and that exactness survives tensor- and sequence-parallel sharding; in the bf16 and 8-bit regimes used in practice it is faithful rather than bit-identical, its deviation bounded and, for the weight store, rendered unbiased by stochastic rounding. Because each gradient tile is born and consumed in the same registers, it is never converted down to bf16 to be stored and read back; FORGE thus preserves the full-precision fidelity that both bf16 and 8-bit optimizers lose to that conversion. Nor is the method tied to one architecture or one optimizer: linear layers are ubiquitous, and FORGE reclaims the gradient memory of any of them under any element-wise rule. Empirically FORGE more than halves the memory of an optimizer step and, at the small batch sizes typical of fine-tuning and continued pretraining, runs about 1.5x faster; integrated into tensor-parallel Megatron-LM it fits 8B training at four times the micro-batch a standard optimizer allows on the same GPUs.

</details>


### [67] [TaLK: Text-attributed Graph Dataset Distillation via Coupling Language Model with Graph-Aware Kernel](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22975)
*Yeongho Kim,Yeonje Choi,Kijung Shin*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TaLK提出了一种针对文本属性图的高效数据集蒸馏方法，通过将语言模型与图感知神经正切核结合，避免重复联合训练，仅用1%的合成数据即可达到全数据集97%的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的文本属性图学习方法通常需要联合训练语言模型和图神经网络，计算开销大且难以扩展；而现有数据集蒸馏方法要么针对单一模态设计，要么在蒸馏过程中仍需在完整数据集上反复训练昂贵的LM-GNN模型，不适合TAG场景。

Method: TaLK将语言模型与图感知神经正切核（graph-aware NTK）耦合，在蒸馏过程中避免对完整数据集进行重复的联合训练，同时保留文本和结构信息，实现高效的TAG数据集蒸馏。

Result: 在多个TAG基准数据集上，TaLK一致优于现有基线方法，仅使用1%的合成数据即可达到全数据集性能的97%。

Conclusion: TaLK通过LM与图感知NTK的耦合设计，有效解决了TAG数据集蒸馏中计算效率与多模态信息保留的平衡问题，为大规模TAG学习提供了可行的低成本方案。

Abstract: Text-attributed graphs (TAGs) are widely used in many real-world domains, and learning on TAGs requires jointly modeling text semantics and graph structure. A standard approach for modeling TAGs is to combine a language model (LM) and a graph neural network (GNN), but joint training is computationally expensive and difficult to scale. Dataset distillation is a promising way to reduce training costs, but existing methods are not well suited to TAGs because they are typically designed for a single modality or still require repeatedly training expensive LM-GNN models on the full dataset during distillation. To address this, we propose TaLK, an effective dataset distillation method for TAGs that couples an LM with a graph-aware neural tangent kernel.This design enables efficient dataset distillation, avoiding repeated joint training on the full dataset while reflecting both textual and structural information for effective TAG learning.Experiments on multiple TAG benchmarks show that TaLK consistently outperforms existing baselines and achieves up to 97% of full-dataset performance with only 1% synthetic data.

</details>


### [68] [EvoRubrics: Dynamic Rubrics as Rewards via Adversarial Co-Evolution for LLM Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23038)
*Hongxin Ding,Baixiang Huang,Yue Fang,Weibin Liao,Zheng Li,Jinyang Zhang,Zhijing Wu,Junfeng Zhao,Yasha Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: EvoRubrics提出了一种策略LLM与评分生成器在每一步训练中进行对抗式共同进化的强化学习框架，使评分标准随策略提升动态调整，解决了静态评分奖励饱和的问题，在多项基准上优于静态和动态评分基线，且完全自监督版本也有显著提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于评分（rubric）的奖励在开放任务中提供可解释、细粒度的优化信号，但预构建的评分在训练过程中保持静态，随着模型策略提升，固定标准逐渐丧失区分能力，导致奖励饱和甚至被钻空子。现有动态评分方法依赖外部前沿模型或标准答案，且更新粒度粗糙。

Method: 提出EvoRubrics，一个共同进化（co-evolution）强化学习框架。框架中包含两个核心组件：策略LLM（Policy LLM）和评分生成器（Rubric Generator），两者在每一步训练中通过对抗式交互共同改进。策略在评分生成器的指导下提升，同时评分生成器自适应调整其标准以保持区分度和信息量，实现评估实时追踪策略，并自然形成自动课程学习。

Result: 在多个基准上，EvoRubrics一致优于静态和动态评分基线。学习到的评分生成器可作为可迁移的奖励模型泛化到新任务。完全自监督变体（无需任何外部监督）也能取得有意义的增益，表明生成与评估之间的共同进化本身即可提供足够丰富的学习信号。

Conclusion: 生成与评估之间的共同进化机制能够有效解决静态评分奖励饱和问题，提供持续的细粒度优化信号，并且仅靠自监督的共同进化就足以产生丰富的学习信号，为开放任务中的强化学习提供了新的范式。

Abstract: Rubric-based rewards offer interpretable and fine-grained optimization signals for reinforcement learning in open-ended tasks where verifiable answers are unavailable. However, pre-constructed rubrics remain static throughout training, creating a fundamental mismatch with the evolving policy: fixed criteria gradually lose discriminative power as the model improves, leading to reward saturation and potential hacking. Recent dynamic rubric methods partially address this but rely on external frontier models or ground-truth answers, and update rubrics only at coarse granularity. We propose EvoRubrics, a co-evolutionary RL framework where a Policy LLM and a Rubric Generator jointly improve through adversarial interaction within each training step. As the policy improves under the rubric generator's guidance, the rubric generator adapts its criteria to remain discriminative and informative, enabling evaluation to track the policy in real time and naturally inducing an automatic curriculum. Experiments show that EvoRubrics consistently outperforms static and dynamic rubric baselines across benchmarks. The learned Rubric Generator further generalizes as a transferable reward model. Notably, even a fully self-supervised variant without any external supervision achieves meaningful gains, suggesting that co-evolution between generation and evaluation alone can provide sufficiently rich learning signals. Our code is publicly available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/EvoRubrics-2155/.

</details>


### [69] [Prime Fourier Embeddings: A Principled Basis for Modular Arithmetic](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23044)
*Hyunsang Hwang,Suhyun Bae,Donghun Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出**Prime Fourier Embeddings (PFE)**，将整数编码为以素数索引的(cos, sin)对，使得模运算归结为选择相关素数通道，无需从零学习代数结构。理论证明：对PFE的乘积群作用等变的线性映射必为每个素数独立的块对角矩阵（Schur引理）；中国剩余定理可预测平方自由复合模数下哪些素数通道与任务相关。实验验证：任务相关/无关通道的专精比超过500倍，所有平方自由复合模数测试均达到完美分布内准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准神经嵌入无法有效暴露数字的代数结构（如模运算），需要模型从数据中隐式发现这些结构。本文旨在设计一种**预先结构化**的嵌入，使代数运算（特别是模运算）在表示层面即可直接完成，降低学习难度。

Method: 1) **Prime Fourier Embeddings (PFE)**：基于有理数域Q的调和分析，将每个整数编码为一系列素数索引的(cos, sin)对。2) 利用**Schur引理**证明：对PFE上的乘积群作用等变的线性映射必为块对角矩阵，每个素数对应一个独立块。3) 通过**中国剩余定理**预测：对于平方自由复合模数，只需关注对应素数因子的通道即可完成任务。

Result: 1) 理论成立：等变线性映射的块对角结构被严格证明。2) 经验验证：任务相关通道与无关通道的专精比超过500倍（即模型几乎只依赖与模数素数因子对应的通道）。3) 所有平方自由复合模数测试上达到**完美分布内准确率**。

Conclusion: PFE通过预置代数结构（素数傅里叶分解）有效解决了神经嵌入对数字代数结构表达能力不足的问题。该方法将模运算从"隐式发现"转变为"显式通道选择"，理论上受群表示论保证，实证上达到完美泛化。工作展示了将经典数论/调和分析工具与神经网络表示设计结合的可行性。

Abstract: Numbers have algebraic structure that standard neural embeddings often fail to expose. We introduce Prime Fourier Embeddings (PFE), which encode integers as prime-indexed (cos, sin) pairs derived from the harmonic analysis of Q, providing a pre-structured representation in which modular arithmetic reduces to selecting the relevant prime channel rather than discovering algebraic structure from scratch. We prove that any linear map equivariant with respect to the product group action on PFE must be block-diagonal with one independent block per prime -- a consequence of Schur's lemma applied to the resulting character decomposition. For square-free composite moduli, the Chinese Remainder Theorem predicts which prime channels are task-relevant. Both predictions are confirmed empirically: ablation studies show specialization ratios exceeding 500x between task-relevant and task-irrelevant channels, with perfect in-distribution test accuracy across all square-free composite moduli tested.

</details>


### [70] [FlowTrain: Flow-Based Decoupled Training for Industrial-Grade Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23087)
*Zhida Jiang,Zhaolong Xing,Yang Pei,Xiaolong Chen,Yuanhang Xiao,Chengzhi Huang,Xiyu Liu,Haopeng Liu,Qingyuan Sang,Lingfeng Zhou,Jiaxing Wang,Zicheng Zhang,Wenzhe Wang,Xinyu Liu,Yan Li,Zhen Chen,Ke Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: FlowTrain提出了一种基于流式解耦的VLM训练框架，通过生产者-消费者数据流和统一内存池实现编码器与主干网络的独立执行，并引入异构并行分配器和动态打包调度器，实现了50%+ MFU和最高1.7倍的吞吐提升，缩小了与纯LLM训练的效率差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业级VLM分布式训练效率远低于单模态LLM训练，现有方案（统一并行设计的单体架构或批同步管线的分离部署）均不够充分，VLM训练可以进一步解耦以提升效率。

Method: FlowTrain将VLM训练重构为生产者-消费者数据流，通过统一内存池协调；编码器和主干网络在全局虚拟地址空间上独立推进；引入异构并行分配器，通过求解吞吐匹配问题分配模块特定的并行策略；使用动态打包调度器根据实际LLM端计算代价在运行时构建均衡的微批次。

Result: 在实际工作负载上，FlowTrain实现了超过50%的MFU（模型浮点利用率），吞吐提升最高达1.7倍，缩小了与纯LLM训练的效率差距。

Conclusion: VLM训练可以通过流式解耦进一步优化效率，FlowTrain通过异构并行和动态打包有效解决了模块间效率不匹配问题，验证了该方向的可行性。

Abstract: Industrial-grade distributed training of vision-language models (VLMs) remains far less efficient than that of unimodal LLMs. Existing solutions either follow a monolithic design that assigns uniform parallelism to heterogeneous modules or adopt a disaggregated deployment that separates modules while executing them as a batch-synchronized pipeline. In this paper, we highlight that the above solutions are still not sufficient, and VLM training can be further decoupled. To this end, we present FlowTrain, a flow-based decoupled training framework that reformulates VLM training as a producer-consumer dataflow coordinated through a unified memory pool. The encoder and backbone can progress independently over a global virtual address space. Since this execution decoupling fundamentally changes the optimization objective of allocation and scheduling, FlowTrain further introduces a heterogeneous parallel allocator that assigns module-specific parallelism strategies by solving a throughput matching problem. The dynamic packing scheduler is used to construct balanced microbatches at runtime according to the actual LLM-side computation cost. Extensive experiments on real-world workloads show that FlowTrain achieves over 50% MFU and up to 1.7x throughput improvement, narrowing the efficiency gap to LLM-only training.

</details>


### [71] [ReNIO: Reweighting Negative Trajectory Importance for LLM On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23104)
*Chen Lin,Kedi Chen,Wei Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现同策略蒸馏中，仅用错误的学生生成样本训练优于仅用正确样本，并提出ReNIO方法，通过学生-教师概率比识别关键错误token并加权，无需最终答案即可强化负轨迹的学习，提升LLM推理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准同策略蒸馏（OPD）平等对待所有学生生成输出，忽略了样本信息量的差异。实验观察到一种持续的对称性：仅用错误输出训练优于仅用正确输出，表明错误输出中蕴含更丰富的探索性推理信号，但现有方法未能有效利用这一信号。

Method: 提出ReNIO（Reweighting Negative trajectory Importance for On-policy distillation），通过学生与教师模型在生成前缀上的概率比，识别导致错误推理轨迹的关键token，聚合为归一化的样本权重，无需观察最终答案正确性即可自动为负轨迹分配更大权重，保留OPD的前缀训练优势。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成任务上，ReNIO改进了OPD和OPSD，代表性相对增益：Qwen3-1.7B在数学推理上提升8.90%，R1-Distill-Qwen-7B提升10.00%。

Conclusion: 错误的学生生成输出比正确输出蕴含更丰富的探索性推理信号，ReNIO通过token级概率比加权有效利用该信号，在无需完整答案rollout的情况下显著提升同策略蒸馏效果。

Abstract: On-policy distillation (OPD) improves LLM reasoning by training a student model on its own generated outputs, but standard OPD treats all student-generated outputs (SGOs) equally regardless of their informativeness. We observe a consistent asymmetry in controlled filtering experiments: in both OPD and on-policy self distillation (OPSD), training only on incorrect SGOs outperforms training only on correct ones. Our further analysis suggests that models trained on correct-only SGOs tend to generate shorter reasoning traces and show weaker reflection behavior, while incorrect SGOs better preserve exploratory reasoning near the model's capability boundary. To exploit this signal without requiring full answer-containing rollouts, we introduce ReNIO, which Reweights Negative trajectory Importance for LLM On-policy distillation. By using the student-to-teacher probability ratio, ReNIO identifies pivotal tokens leading to wrong reasoning traces and aggregates their information into a normalized sample weight, inherently assigning larger weights to likely negative trajectories without observing the correctness of final-answer. Since Re-NIO only uses prefix-conditioned token probabilities, it preserves OPD's prefix training advantage over full-rollout reinforcement learning. Across both mathematical reasoning and code generation tasks, ReNIO improves both OPD and OPSD, with representative relative gains of up to 8.90% for Qwen3-1.7B and 10.00% for R1-Distill-Qwen-7B on mathematical reasoning benchmarks. Code repo: https://github.com/BDML-lab/ReNIO.

</details>


### [72] [Memory Contagion: Cross-Temporal Propagation of Evaluator Bias via Agent Memory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23195)
*Zewen Liu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发现并形式化了一种新现象——记忆传染（Memory Contagion）：当LLM智能体的经验受到有偏评估者的影响时，这种偏差会通过记忆系统跨时间传播给未来的智能体，即使在完美整合（oracle）条件下也无法避免。实验表明，偏差传播在低至20%的污染率下即可发生，且整合对不同偏差类型有相反效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究假设智能体记忆源于无偏经验，但实际中智能体常受有偏评估者训练或指导。作者旨在揭示并形式化这种评估者偏差通过记忆系统跨时间传播的现象，暴露当前智能体记忆设计的脆弱性。

Method: 定义记忆传染现象，设计两种偏差类型（长度偏好、权威偏差）和四个实验阶段。设置oracle条件（完美整合）作为对照，并测试不同污染率（低至p=0.2）下的偏差传播效果。

Result: (1)即使完美整合条件下，记忆传染仍然发生，证明有偏输入是充分原因；(2)整合对不同偏差类型效果相反：有效削弱长度偏差，但初步放大权威偏差；(3)无安全阈值：污染率低至20%即可检测到偏差传播。

Conclusion: 记忆传染揭示了当前智能体记忆设计的关键漏洞，作者提供了衡量跨时间偏差传播的形式化工具，为构建更鲁棒的智能体记忆系统奠定基础。

Abstract: Large Language Model (LLM) agents increasingly rely on memory systems to maintain long-term coherence. Recent work shows that agent memories degrade during continuous consolidation. However, existing research assumes memories are derived from unbiased experiences. In this work, we identify and formalize a novel phenomenon: Memory Contagion -- the cross-temporal propagation of evaluator bias through agent memory. We show that when agents are trained or guided by biased evaluators, their experiences become biased; when these trajectories are stored and consolidated into memory, the bias propagates to future agents retrieving from the same memory store, even when consolidation is perfect (oracle). Across two bias types (length preference, authority bias) and four experimental phases, we demonstrate: (1) Memory Contagion occurs even with perfect consolidation (oracle condition), proving that biased input is a sufficient cause of contagion; (2) Consolidation has opposite effects depending on bias type -- robustly attenuating length bias while preliminarily amplifying authority bias (single-run estimate), suggesting a bias-type-dependent interaction; (3) No observed safe threshold: bias propagation is detected at contamination rates as low as p=0.2. Our findings expose a critical vulnerability in current agent memory designs and provide formal tools for measuring cross-temporal bias propagation.

</details>


### [73] [Exposing the Illusion of Erasure in Knowledge Editing for LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23276)
*Advik Raj Basani,Anshuman Chhabra*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文从对抗性诱导视角研究知识编辑（KE），发现编辑后的知识并未真正擦除，而是重新分布在表示空间中；低秩更新仅起到抑制原始事实表达的作用，而非移除知识；编辑后的知识位于狭窄各向异性的损失盆地中，易受扰动和对抗攻击，证明KE算法本质上可被绕过。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识编辑（KE）已成为更新LLM中特定事实的前沿方法，但其可靠性和底层机制尚不清楚，本文旨在揭示KE实际工作原理及其脆弱性。

Method: 采用对抗性诱导视角进行实证评估，结合机制分析（低秩更新的表示空间分布）、损失景观分析等方法，在多种模型架构上进行系统性实验。

Result: 编辑知识并未被擦除而是重新分布；低秩更新仅抑制而非移除知识；编辑后知识位于狭窄各向异性区域，极易受间接提示和对抗攻击影响；多种架构上存在一致的失败模式。

Conclusion: KE算法本质上可被绕过，揭示了架构层面的根本性漏洞，需重新审视后验更新在LLM应用中的部署方式。

Abstract: Knowledge Editing (KE) has emerged as a frontier for updating specific facts in LLMs without costly retraining, but its reliability and underlying mechanisms remain poorly understood. In this work, we examine KE from an adversarial elicitation perspective, revealing that edited knowledge is often not fully erased and continues to surface, with consistent failures observed across diverse model architectures. To explain this behavior, we conduct a mechanistic analysis of popular KE methods. We show that low-rank updates do not overwrite existing knowledge but instead redistribute it within the model's representation space. Furthermore, we find that these methods act as targeted suppression mechanisms that reduce the likelihood of expressing original facts, rather than removing them from the model. Analysis of the loss landscape reveals that edited knowledge lies in narrow, anisotropic regions that are highly sensitive to perturbations, making them highly vulnerable to indirect prompting and adversarial attacks. By exposing these profound architectural vulnerabilities, our work proves that KE algorithms are inherently bypassable and motivates a fundamental reevaluation of how we deploy post-hoc updates in several LLM applications.

</details>


### [74] [GRIMIP: A General Framework for Instance-Specific Configuration of MIP Solvers Using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23299)
*Yidong Luo,Xuemin Chen,Chenguang Wang,Fangzhou Zhu,Tao Zhong,Tianshu Yu*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRIMIP是一个混合智能框架，将大语言模型的语义推理能力与贝叶斯优化的高效搜索相结合，用于自动配置混合整数规划求解器的超参数，在MIPLIB等七个基准上对困难实例实现了超40%的Primal-Dual Integral降低，性能优于SMAC及其他LLM辅助BO方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MIP求解器的超参数配置是高维度、实例依赖的优化问题，次优设置可能使求解时间增加数量级。默认配置通常次优，传统调优方法要么存在冷启动问题且搜索效率低，要么严重依赖专家经验。

Method: 提出GRIMIP框架，将LLM作为贝叶斯优化循环中的完整概率代理模型（probabilistic surrogate），协同融合LLM的语义推理能力与BO的样本高效搜索能力，给予LLM充分自主性。

Result: 在包含MIPLIB在内的七个基准测试上，GRIMIP对困难实例实现了超过40%的Primal-Dual Integral降低，性能优于SMAC及其他LLM辅助BO方法。

Conclusion: GRIMIP通过将LLM的专家级推理与BO的高效搜索相结合，实现了最先进的性能，显著降低了采样和评估成本，有效解决了MIP求解器超参数配置的冷启动和搜索效率问题。

Abstract: Configuring the hyperparameters of Mixed-integer programming (MIP) solvers is a high-dimensional, instance-dependent optimization problem where suboptimal settings can degrade solving time by orders of magnitude. Default configurations are often suboptimal, while traditional tuning methods either suffer from the ``cold-start'' problem and inefficient search or heavily rely on expert experience. This paper introduces \textbf{GRIMIP} (\textbf{\underline{G}}eneral \textbf{\underline{R}}easoning for \textbf{\underline{I}}nstance-specific \textbf{\underline{MIP}} configuration), a novel hybrid intelligence framework that synergistically integrates the semantic reasoning capabilities of Large Language Models (LLMs) with the sample-efficient search of Bayesian Optimization (BO). GRIMIP enables the LLM to function as a complete probabilistic surrogate within the BO loop, significantly improving performance and reducing sampling and evaluation costs. On seven benchmarks including MIPLIB, GRIMIP achieves over 40\% reduction in Primal-Dual Integral on hard instances, outperforming SMAC and other LLM-assisted BO methods. By granting LLMs sufficient autonomy, GRIMIP combines the expert-level reasoning of LLMs with the efficient search of BO, achieving state-of-the-art performance.

</details>


### [75] [Superhuman AI for Generals.io Using Self-Play Reinforcement Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23348)
*Matej Straka,Viliam Lisý,Martin Schmid*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在Generals.io即时策略游戏中达到超人类水平的AI智能体，通过JAX原生模拟器实现每秒数千万帧的10,000倍速度提升，结合视觉Transformer策略网络、自对弈策略梯度训练、top-advantage样本过滤和指数移动平均参数更新，在4×NVIDIA H200 GPU上训练4天后，击败了5,000多名人类玩家中的第一名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不完全信息、需要长时规划与短期战术结合的即时策略游戏Generals.io中，构建超人类AI智能体，并探究当快速模拟器消除数据瓶颈后，哪些因素真正重要。

Method: 使用JAX原生的高速模拟器（单GPU每秒数千万帧，比之前快10,000倍）；基于视觉Transformer的策略网络；通过自对弈策略梯度循环训练，使用稀疏的胜负奖励；采用top-advantage样本过滤和策略参数的指数移动平均。

Result: 在4×NVIDIA H200 GPU上训练4天后，在5,000多名人类玩家的公开1v1排行榜上达到第一名；与第二名差距相当于第二名到第25名的差距；在269场天梯比赛中以199-70的总比分击败两位顶级人类玩家。

Conclusion: 快速模拟器消除数据瓶颈后，本文方法揭示了在训练中什么因素真正重要、什么因素不重要，强调了基础设施加速在AI训练中的关键作用。

Abstract: We present a superhuman AI agent for Generals.io, a real-time strategy game that requires both long-horizon planning and short-term tactics under strong imperfect information. Trained for four days on 4x NVIDIA H200 GPUs, our agent reaches #1 on the public 1v1 leaderboard of over 5,000 human players, leading the second-ranked player by the same margin that separates second place from 25th, and beats the two top-ranked humans head-to-head with a combined 199-70 record across 269 ladder matches. A key enabler is a JAX-native simulator that reaches tens of millions of frames per second on a single GPU, roughly a 10,000x speedup over the prior simulator. On top of this, we train a vision transformer policy end-to-end by self-play with a policy-gradient loop and sparse win/loss reward, using top-advantage sample filtering and an exponential moving average of the policy parameters. Taken together, our findings highlight what matters, and what does not, once a fast simulator removes the data bottleneck.

</details>


### [76] [Distribution-Aware Diffusion-LLM for Robust Ultra-Long-Term Time Series Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23391)
*Falguni Ghosh,Vahid Hashemi,Bernhard Kainz*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出Diffusion-LLM框架，将条件扩散模型与LLM结合，用于时间序列预测，在六个长期预测基准上取得优于现有LLM基线的结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在多模态时间序列预测中面临两个挑战：缺乏对非文本数据的校准概率建模，以及难以对齐异构表示。

Method: 将条件扩散模型集成到LLM预测流程中，联合学习未来数据的条件分布，同时在共享潜在空间中改进语义对齐。

Result: 在ETT、Weather、ECL等六个长期预测基准上一致优于现有LLM基线，在超长期和少样本预测中表现尤为突出。

Conclusion: 分布感知的正则化能有效增强时间序列LLM的鲁棒性和泛化能力。

Abstract: Time series forecasting is a fundamental machine learning task. Recent work has explored Large Language Models (LLMs) for this purpose due to their strong generalization, pattern recognition, and zero-shot or few-shot capabilities. Despite their suitability for long-context learning, LLMs face challenges in multimodal settings: they lack calibrated probabilistic modeling for non-text data and struggle to align heterogeneous representations. To address these issues, we propose a new framework Diffusion-LLM that integrates a conditional diffusion model into an LLM-based forecasting pipeline. This joint design enables learning the conditional distribution of future data while improving semantic alignment in a shared latent space. We evaluate Diffusion-LLM on six long-term forecasting benchmarks, including ETT, Weather, and ECL. Our method consistently outperforms existing LLM-based baseline, achieving notable gains in ultra-long-term and few-shot forecasting and demonstrating the value of distribution-aware regularization for enhancing robustness and generalization in time series LLMs.

</details>


### [77] [HyperQuant: A Rate-Distortion-Optimal Quantization Pipeline for Large Language and Diffusion Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23406)
*Yuval Domb,Hadar Sackstein,Tomer Solberg*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: HyperQuant是一个统一的训练后量化流水线，用于大语言模型和扩散Transformer的权重及KV缓存。它结合了随机化Hadamard变换、最优格量化、熵接近最优的Rice编码和KV缓存偏差校正四项技术，在3-5 bps权重量化和低至1.7 bps KV量化上均优于现有方案，在H100上实现约3.9x权重压缩和约3.79x KV缓存压缩，且质量接近无损。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 提出一种统一的、高性能的训练后量化方案，同时高效压缩大语言模型和扩散Transformer的权重与KV缓存，克服现有量化方案在低比特率下的精度损失问题，并支持多种硬件加速路径。

Method: HyperQuant的四步流水线：(i) 逐块的随机化Hadamard变换，使权重和激活的逐坐标分布近似高斯；(ii) 量化到低维最优格（E8, D4, A2, Z）；(iii) 无损比特剥离和近熵最优的变长Rice编码；(iv) KV缓存的偏差校正方法，保持内积下的无偏重构，保留注意力语义。集成支持fp8-e4m3、int8、nvfp4、mxfp4等Tensor-Core MMA路径。

Result: 在多个自包含实验中，HyperQuant在3-5 bps权重量化上全面优于HIGGS，在低至1.7 bps的KV量化上优于TurboQuant和OCTOPUS。对19B参数的LTX-2 DiT视频模型量化未出现可观察的逐帧伪影。在H100上4 bps时，线性权重压缩约3.9x，KV缓存压缩约3.79x，接近无损质量。int8在RHT后的格输出上表现优于fp8。

Conclusion: HyperQuant通过融合四项已知技术，构建了一个统一且高效的量化流水线，在权重和KV缓存量化上均达到或超越当前最优水平，并验证了在扩散Transformer上的适用性，为实际部署提供了高性能、低比特的量化方案。

Abstract: We present HyperQuant (Hadamard, optimallY Packing, Entropy Rice-coding), a unified post-training quantization pipeline for the weights and the KV cache of large language and diffusion transformers. Across a suite of self-contained experiments (Table 1), HyperQuant outperforms the recent HIGGS scheme at every operating point from 3 to 5 bits per scalar (bps) on weights, and beats both TurboQuant and OCTOPUS on KV quantization down to 1.7 bps. Beyond the LLM setting, HyperQuant quantizes the 19B-parameter LTX-2 DiT video model with no observable per-frame artifacts. End-to-end on an H100 at 4 bps, HyperQuant compresses the linear weights ~3.9x and the KV cache ~3.79x at near-lossless quality.
  HyperQuant combines four known ideas into a single construction: (i) a per-tile Randomized Hadamard Transform that makes the per-coordinate distribution of weights and activations approximately Gaussian; (ii) quantization to a low-dimensional optimal lattice (E8, D4, A2, or Z); (iii) lossless bit-stripping and near-entropy-optimal variable-length Rice coding of the lattice indices; and (iv) bias-correction methods for the KV cache that keep the reconstruction unbiased under inner products, preserving attention semantics. We further integrate the pipeline with 8-bit and 4-bit Tensor-Core MMA paths (fp8-e4m3, int8, nvfp4, mxfp4), and find that int8 beats fp8 on the post-RHT lattice output. Project page: https://moonmath.ai/hyperquant/

</details>


### [78] [GRINQH: Graded Input-based Quantization Hierarchy for Efficient LLM Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23419)
*Jette Oberländer,Jan Finkbeiner,Catherine M. Schöfmann,Emre Neftci*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: GRINQH是一种面向边缘计算场景的权重-only训练后量化框架，通过结合量化和稀疏化，利用激活幅度作为重要性代理动态分配权重通道到不同精度级别，在解码阶段实现灵活的平均位宽，在Llama3和Qwen3模型上以3/4-bit设置超越现有方法，并支持2-bit生成，建立了生成质量与推理速度之间的新帕累托前沿。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM自回归解码主要受限于GPU显存带宽，尤其在边缘计算场景中。现有量化方法将推理视为均匀过程，未能区分计算受限的预填充阶段和显存受限的解码阶段之间的不对称性。

Method: 提出GRINQH框架，权重-only训练后量化，利用激活幅度作为计算重要性的代理指标，动态将权重通道分配到不同精度级别，实现解码时的灵活平均位宽，并采用分层嵌套内存布局进行多精度存储，结合自定义GPU kernel实现理论加速。

Result: 在Llama3和Qwen3模型上，GRINQH在3-bit和4-bit设置下优于现有固定精度和混合精度基线，并能实现有效的2-bit生成，通过实验验证了理论加速。

Conclusion: GRINQH建立了LLM生成领域新的帕累托前沿，实现了生成质量与推理速度之间的动态权衡。

Abstract: Autoregressive decoding with LLMs is primarily bottlenecked by GPU memory bandwidth, especially in edge-computing settings. While quantization is essential for mitigating this bottleneck, most existing methods treat inference as a uniform process and fail to account for the asymmetry between the compute-bound prefill stage and the memory-bound decoding stage. We propose GRINQH (GRaded INput-based Quantization Hierarchy), a weight-only post-training quantization framework that accelerates decoding by unifying quantization and sparsification. GRINQH leverages activation magnitudes as a proxy for computational importance to dynamically assign weight channels to different precision levels, enabling flexible average bit widths during decoding. Evaluated on Llama3 and Qwen3 models, GRINQH outperforms state-of-the-art fixed- and mixed-precision baselines at comparable 3- and 4-bit settings, even enabling effective 2-bit generation. We experimentally verify theoretical speedups by leveraging a hierarchical nested memory layout for multi-precision storage in a custom GPU kernel. Ultimately, GRINQH establishes a new state-of-the-art Pareto frontier for LLM generation, enabling a dynamic trade-off between generation quality and inference speed.

</details>


### [79] [TROPT: An Open Framework for Unifying and Advancing Discrete Text Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23496)
*Matan Ben-Tov,Mahmood Sharif*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: TROPT是一个统一离散文本优化器的开源框架，集成了30+优化配方、15+优化器和15+损失函数，覆盖越狱攻击、模型探测等多个应用领域，显著降低了离散文本优化的使用和发展门槛。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有离散文本优化器分散在不同的研究代码库中，难以直接比较和复用；优化器变体繁多，工程开销大，阻碍了离散优化技术的采用和进步。

Method: 提出TROPT框架，通过统一接口标准化不同离散优化器的执行流程，支持灵活替换模型、目标和优化器等组件，集成超过30种优化配方，涵盖白盒到黑盒的15+优化器和15+损失函数。

Result: 通过大规模对比实验发现了一些有效但未被充分采用的越狱优化技术；成功将优化器从一个领域（如LLM越狱）迁移到新领域（如语料投毒嵌入模型）。

Conclusion: TROPT框架显著降低了离散文本优化技术的采用和发展门槛，为红队测试、审计和可解释性研究提供了统一工具平台。

Abstract: Discrete text-trigger optimization -- searching for text sequences that, when ingested by a model, steer it toward a specified objective -- underpins model red-teaming (e.g., LLM jailbreaks), as well as auditing and interpretability. However, the current state of discrete optimizers hinders their adoption and progress. First, existing optimizers, when open-sourced at all, are scattered across research codebases tied to specific models, objectives, and problem domains. Second, optimizer variants proliferate, each requiring engineering overhead to use or extend, and remaining hard to compare head-to-head. Together, these raise the bar for adopting optimizers in existing or new domains, and for advancing them via new strategies. We address these gaps with TROPT, the first open-source framework that unifies discrete optimizers' execution and standardizes their development under a single interface. TROPT makes it easy to customize end-to-end optimization recipes by swapping any component -- models, objectives, and optimizers -- extending its reach across domains and new applications. TROPT currently ships with 30+ optimization recipes -- covering applications such as jailbreaking and probing model internals -- built from 15+ optimizers (spanning white-box to black-box access) and 15+ losses, from foundational to state-of-the-art methods. Demonstrating its utility, we leverage TROPT in several studies: (i) controlled, large-scale experiments comparing and enhancing optimization strategies for LLM jailbreaks, revealing potent-yet-underadopted techniques; and (ii) porting optimizers from one domain (e.g., LLM jailbreak) to new domains (e.g., corpus-poisoning embedding model). In all, TROPT significantly lowers the barrier to adopting and advancing discrete text optimization.

</details>


### [80] [SVD-Surgeon: Optimal Singular-Value Surgery for Large Language Model Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23568)
*Mahmoud Safari,Frank Hutter*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: SVD-Surgeon是一种无需训练的SVD压缩增强方法，通过将最优脑外科医生框架引入奇异值基，对被截断的奇异值损失进行二阶闭环补偿更新，显著提升LLM压缩性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型部署受限于显存和计算需求，现有SVD低秩压缩方法仅关注如何分解和保留哪些成分，缺乏对被截断奇异值造成损失的补偿机制。

Method: 将OBS框架引入奇异值基，将每个奇异值视为独立参数，对被截断的奇异值导致的模型损失进行二阶近似补偿，计算保留奇异值的闭式更新量；同时基于相同分析计算奇异值的剪枝显著性。

Result: 在SVD-LLM基础上应用SVD-Surgeon，无需重新训练即可在OPT系列和LLaMA 2-7B上改善困惑度-压缩权衡曲线。

Conclusion: SVD-Surgeon可作为一种即插即用的增强模块叠加于现有SVD压缩方法之上，在不增加训练开销的前提下提升压缩质量。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) achieve remarkable performance across a wide range of tasks, but their deployment is constrained by substantial memory and compute requirements. Low-rank compression via singular value decomposition (SVD) is an effective remedy, but existing methods focus on how to factorize and which components to keep. We introduce SVD-Surgeon, a training-free method that brings the Optimal Brain Surgeon (OBS) framework to the singular-value basis. Treating each singular value as a parameter, it computes a closed-form update of the retained singular values that compensates, to second order in the model loss, for those removed by truncation. The same analysis yields a saliency for choosing which values to prune. As it operates directly on the singular-value factorization, SVD-Surgeon can be layered on top of existing SVD compressors. Applied to SVD-LLM, a leading SVD-based method, it improves the perplexity-compression trade-off on the OPT family and LLaMA 2-7B without any retraining.

</details>


### [81] [Quantifying the Agreement Between Data-Influence and Data-Similarity to Understand LLM Behavior](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23591)
*Christopher J. Anders,Henrique Da Silva Gameiro,Nico Daheim,Mohammad Emtiyaz Khan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统比较了两种用于追踪LLM输出到训练数据的方法——数据相似度（data-similarity）和数据影响力（data-influence），发现两者排序高度一致但存在不对称性：数据相似度的顶部文档在数据影响力下排名更稳定。利用这种不对称性，可以用昂贵的数据影响力来优化廉价的数据相似度结果，实现成本-精度的良好权衡。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管数据相似度和数据影响力两种方法被广泛用于LLM输出追踪，但缺乏对两者在输出追踪任务上的系统性比较。作者希望填补这一空白，精确量化两者的共性与差异。

Method: 首先分别根据数据相似度和数据影响力对训练文档进行排序，然后计算两个排序之间的重叠程度。实验在OLMo2-1B、Qwen3-1.7B、LlaMa3.2-1B、Gemma3-1B和GPT2多个模型上进行。

Result: 两种排序显著一致，但存在不对称性：数据相似度的顶部文档被数据影响力赋予的排名更一致（即更稳定），反之则不成立。利用这种不对称性，可以用昂贵的数据影响力来精炼廉价的数据相似度结果，获得更优的成本-精度权衡。

Conclusion: 数据相似度和数据影响力在输出追踪任务中高度一致但存在不对称关系，利用这一特性可以在保持精度的同时降低成本。

Abstract: One way to understand LLM behavior is to trace its output back to the training data. Two types of measures are commonly used for output tracing: data-similarity and data-influence. The former is cheaper while the latter is believed to be more accurate. Even though many works have compared them for ground-truth tasks, no such comparisons exist for output tracing. Here, we fill this gap and precisely quantify the commonalities and differences between the two measures. We do this by first ranking the training documents according to each measure and then computing the overlap between the two rankings. Our main finding is that the two rankings agree significantly, but there is an asymmetry between them: The top documents of data-similarity are assigned more consistent ranks by data-influence than the other way around. This result is valid across a range of experiments involving OLMo2-1B, Qwen3-1.7B, LlaMa3.2-1B, Gemma3-1B, and GPT2. We exploit the asymmetry to obtain a favorable cost-accuracy trade-off by using the costly data-influence to refine the results of data-similarity.

</details>


### [82] [MAS-PromptBench: When Does Prompt Optimization Improve Multi-Agent LLM Systems?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23664)
*Juyang Bai,Laixi Shi*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了多智能体系统（MAS）中系统提示词优化的效果，发现其能显著提升性能，但优化效果高度依赖于任务、工作流、通信协议和团队规模等系统配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管提示词优化在单一LLM上展现了巨大潜力，但在多智能体系统（MAS）中面临搜索空间指数级增长的挑战，目前尚不清楚提示词优化在何时、多大程度上能提升MAS性能，以及其收益对系统配置的敏感程度。

Method: 在多种MAS设置（不同任务、工作流、通信协议和团队规模）下系统性地研究系统提示词优化，将两种最先进的单智能体提示词优化方法扩展到MAS场景作为基准优化器。

Result: 揭示了提示词优化在MAS中解锁显著性能增益的潜力，同时暴露了开放挑战；明确了在不同MAS设置下提示词优化何时以及多大程度上有助于提升性能。

Conclusion: 提示词优化对MAS性能提升有效但具有高度情境依赖性，其增益大小受系统配置因素显著影响，需要针对具体场景进行适配优化。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems (MAS) offer a scalable path forward for agentic AI, comprising multiple LLM-based agents, each assigned a system prompt and a position within a workflow that governs inter-agent coordination and output aggregation. System prompts thus form a critical and accessible optimization surface: they specify agents' roles and behaviors, enabling system-level improvements without model finetuning. Although prompt optimization has shown substantial potential for single LLMs, extending it to MAS poses distinct challenges, notably an exponentially growing search space. It remains unclear whether, when, and by how much prompt optimization improves MAS performance, and how sensitive such gains are to system configuration. In this work, we systematically study system-prompt optimization across a broad range of MAS setups varying in task, workflow, communication protocol, and team size, benchmarking two prompt optimizers that naturally extend state-of-the-art single-agent methods. The results reveal its potential to unlock significant gains while exposing open challenges, characterizing when and how much prompt optimization helps across diverse MAS settings.

</details>


### [83] [On the Limits of Prompt-Conditioned Language Models as General-Purpose Learners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23668)
*David Mguni,Julian Ma,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文通过廉价对话博弈论框架分析提示学习，证明语言作为容量受限的通信通道会导致任务不可区分性（表达性下限）和对齐约束导致的目标偏差下限，从而得出提示条件化LLM无法通过提示成为通用问题求解器的形式化否定结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 反驳LLM被视为通用求解器的流行观点，揭示语言作为任务信息传递接口存在根本性容量限制，并形式化证明这种限制会导致不可消除的性能误差下限。

Method: 将用户-系统交互建模为双层廉价对话博弈，分离任务推理与执行过程，推导PAC-Bayes界限来区分有限样本估计误差与不可约的结构限制。

Result: 两个核心结果：(1) 表达性下限：任务族的信息复杂度超过语言通道容量时，不同任务变得不可区分，产生无法通过数据、优化或缩放消除的误差；(2) 目标偏差下限：对齐约束导致用户理想分布不可实现，产生不可约失真。

Conclusion: 提示条件化LLM在无限数据下也无法实现通用问题求解；超越自然语言的接口（多模态、外部记忆）可能缓解这些限制。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are frequently portrayed as general-purpose solvers capable of solving arbitrary tasks. We argue that this view overlooks a fundamental constraint: language is a compressed and capacity-limited interface for conveying task information. Modelling User--System interaction as a bilevel \emph{cheap-talk} game, we analyse how latent tasks are encoded into prompts and reinterpreted under alignment and safety constraints. We introduce a conceptual decomposition separating task inference from execution and derive PAC-Bayes bounds that distinguish finite-sample estimation error from irreducible structural limitations. Our first main result establishes an \emph{expressivity floor}: language acts as a capacity-limited communication channel, and whenever the informational complexity of a task family exceeds the capacity of that channel, distinct tasks become unavoidably indistinguishable to the Solver, inducing a strictly positive error floor that cannot be eliminated by additional data, optimisation, or model scaling alone. We then establish an \emph{objective-misalignment floor}: when alignment constraints restrict the admissible output set, the User-ideal distribution may lie outside the feasible class, inducing an irreducible distortion. Together, these results yield a formal negative conclusion: prompt-conditioned LLMs are not universal problem solvers through prompting alone, as there exist task families for which correct behaviour is provably unattainable even in the infinite-data regime. More broadly, our analysis shows the limits of prompt-based generalisation arise from information-constrained communication and alignment-constrained objectives. This suggests that interfaces beyond natural language, including multimodal observations and, external memory, may reduce the inherent LLM limitations by increasing the task-relevant information available to the System.

</details>


### [84] [Open Problem: Is AdamW Effective Under Heavy-Tailed Noise?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23676)
*Dingzhi Yu,Hongyi Tao,Yuanyu Wan,Luo Luo,Lijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文指出，尽管AdamW是大语言模型训练的事实标准优化器，但其在重尾噪声（即随机梯度噪声具有重尾分布）环境下的收敛理论尚未建立。作者将这一问题形式化为开放问题，给出了一个正加权度量基准，并通过走廊下界机制揭示了分母记忆如何隐藏大梯度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有理论主要基于有限方差假设，但LLM预训练中随机梯度噪声通常是重尾的。虽然Sign-based优化器（如Lion、Muon）和AdaGrad已在重尾噪声下被证明收敛，但AdamW在这一重要场景下的严格收敛理论仍然缺失。

Method: 将AdamW在重尾噪声下的收敛性形式化为开放问题；证明一个正加权度量基准；设计走廊下界机制，分析AdamW的二阶矩累积器（分母记忆）如何可能阻碍对大梯度的正确感知。

Result: 未给出AdamW能否收敛的最终结论，但通过理论分析揭示了分母记忆可能导致大梯度被隐藏这一潜在障碍，并为后续研究提供了基准框架和下界机制。

Conclusion: AdamW在重尾噪声下的收敛性仍是一个开放问题；其分母记忆机制可能构成不同于Sign-based优化器的独特挑战，需要进一步理论探索。

Abstract: AdamW is the de facto optimizer for training large language models (LLMs), yet the theory behind it still lives mostly in finite-variance regimes. This is increasingly unsatisfying, as empirical evidence indicates that stochastic gradient noise in LLM pretraining is typically heavy-tailed. Recent work shows that sign-based optimizers such as Lion and Muon achieve sharp heavy-tailed rates, and that AdaGrad can also converge under heavy-tailed noise. However, no rigorous convergence theory for AdamW has yet been established in this regime. Can AdamW converge under the same heavy-tailed assumptions, or does its second-moment accumulator create a genuine obstruction? We formulate this as an open problem, prove a positive weighted-metric benchmark, and give a corridor lower-bound mechanism showing how denominator memory can hide large gradients.

</details>

### [85] [LLM-Guided Test-Time Discovery of Quantum-Chemical Approximation Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20729)
*Masaya Hagai,Yuta Suzuki,Tomoya Murata,Shuhei Kurita,Masaki Adachi*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: LADeQ是一个由LLM驱动的工作流，能够在现有量子化学代码中动态发现、实现和测试近似算法，无需预训练或特定数据集，并在CCSD和CISD计算中实现了加速且误差可控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子化学模拟受限于高昂的计算成本和固定的近似方案；现有基础模型依赖大规模预训练，在化学空间前沿适应性差；智能体AI系统受限于预定义工具和算法。

Method: 提出LADeQ，利用现成的大语言模型，在测试时从空间统计、电路仿真、核方法等学科中按需构建候选近似方案，直接集成到现有量子化学代码中。

Result: LADeQ加速了CCSD和CISD计算，同时将相关能量误差控制在用户指定的容忍范围内。

Conclusion: 展示了在现有电子结构求解器内部实现自主、目标驱动的近似算法发现是可行的，为量子化学模拟提供了一种灵活、可解释且误差可追踪的新范式。

Abstract: Quantum chemistry simulations underpin modern materials discovery, yet their impact is limited by steep computational cost and dependence on fixed approximation schemes. Foundation models, such as machine-learned interatomic potentials, have accelerated parts of this workflow, but their reliance on large-scale pretraining restricts adaptability at the frontier of chemical space, where methodological innovation and sparse data are the norm. Agentic AI systems can automate existing simulation pipelines, yet they remain constrained by the predefined tools and algorithms they orchestrate. In response, we introduce LADeQ, an LLM-guided workflow that discovers, implements, and benchmarks candidate approximation algorithms at test-time within existing quantum chemistry codes. Rather than selecting from a predefined repertoire, LADeQ constructs candidate approximation schemes on demand, drawing on techniques from disciplines such as spatial statistics, circuit simulation, and kernel methods that have had little prior presence in electronic-structure theory. Because it builds on an out-of-the-box language model, LADeQ requires no task-specific pretraining or curated data, and the resulting implementations are transparent and inspectable, with explicitly traceable approximation errors that enable principled control of accuracy--efficiency trade-offs. We show that LADeQ accelerates coupled cluster singles and doubles (CCSD) and configuration interaction singles and doubles (CISD) calculations while keeping correlation-energy errors within user-specified tolerances, demonstrating autonomous, objective-driven discovery of approximation algorithms inside existing electronic-structure solvers.

</details>


### [86] [Empowering Polymeric Materials Discovery by Artificial Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20753)
*Chenyao Ma,Linda Zhang,Yuheng Chen,Wei Du,Shangwen Fang,Zihao Jiang,Chuanyu Liu,Xinyu Ma,Rui Su,Gang Wang,Muyao Yu,Dong Zhong,Jie Zhu,Weibo Gong,Huan Gu,Limin Li,Chen Shen,Rui Wu,Zhenghao Wu,Kan Xu,Min Zhou,Donglin He,Xiayun Huang,Shan Jiang,Pengfei Ou,Jiayu Peng,Yuwei Zhang,Jie Zhao,Di Zhang,Piao Ma,Zhenghao Li,Hao Li*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 高分子科学正进入自主发现时代，通过数据、模拟、推理和实验的自改进反馈循环，实现从分子设计到工业转化的全链条自主创新。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高分子材料的理性设计因分子组成、链结构、加工历史与多尺度结构演化之间的复杂相互作用而极具挑战性，依赖劳动密集型实验和碎片化建模方法严重限制了机理理解和创新效率。

Method: 整合高分子数据库、机器学习、大AI模型和自动化实验室，构建互联自主发现生态系统，形成数据、模拟、推理与实验之间的自改进反馈循环，实现假设生成、材料设计、实验执行和模型优化的闭环迭代。

Result: 核心挑战从仅提升预测精度转向实现可靠决策、自适应学习以及计算、实验和科学推理的无缝集成，建立起更具预测性、可重复性和可扩展性的高分子创新范式。

Conclusion: 高分子科学正迈向自主发现时代，统一分子设计、工艺优化、实验验证与工业转化，从根本上变革高分子研究方式。

Abstract: Polymeric materials underpin modern technologies spanning energy storage, microelectronics, healthcare and sustainable manufacturing. Yet their rational design remains exceptionally challenging because material performance emerges from complex interactions among molecular composition, chain architecture, processing history and hierarchical structural evolution across multiple length and time scales. Consequently, polymer research has long relied on labor-intensive experimentation and fragmented modeling approaches, limiting both mechanistic understanding and innovation efficiency. Recent advances in data infrastructure, machine learning, large artificial intelligence (AI) models and laboratory automation are beginning to reshape this landscape. Rather than functioning as isolated tools, polymer databases, predictive models, AI agents and automated laboratories are increasingly converging into interconnected discovery ecosystems. As a result, the central challenge is shifting from improving predictive accuracy alone to enabling reliable decision-making, adaptive learning and seamless integration across computation, experimentation and scientific reasoning. We argue that polymer science is entering an era of autonomous discovery, in which data, simulation, reasoning and experimentation operate within self-improving feedback loops that continuously generate hypotheses, design materials, execute experiments and refine predictive models. By unifying molecular design, process optimization, experimental validation and industrial translation, such autonomous ecosystems establish a more predictive, reproducible and scalable paradigm for polymer innovation, fundamentally transforming how polymer research is conducted.

</details>


### [87] [A large-scale foundation model enables simulation-to-real adaptation for nuclear magnetic resonance-based molecular structure analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20756)
*Chen Yang,Zheng Fang,Hanyu Sun,Fanjie Xu,Hongxin Xiang,Hanyu Gao,Xiangxiang Zeng,Yuqiang Li,Xiaojian Wang,Jun Xia*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: UltraNMR是一个大规模核磁共振光谱基础模型，利用1.58亿对模拟¹H和¹³C NMR光谱进行预训练，通过捕获谱内和谱间依赖关系实现模拟到真实的迁移，在多种分子结构分析任务上取得最优性能，并成功应用于中药天然产物结构解析。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验NMR数据集的稀缺限制了深度学习在该领域的泛化能力，现有方法多为窄范围、任务特定的应用，缺乏通用性。

Method: 收集1.58亿对模拟¹H和¹³C NMR光谱，采用多个领域特定的预训练目标训练UltraNMR，捕获谱内和谱间依赖关系，实现模拟到真实的迁移学习。

Result: 在多种实验NMR光谱的分子结构分析任务上达到最先进性能；构建覆盖9400万独特分子的NMR光谱向量库，实现高效结构感知检索；成功解析《中国药典》中两种中药天然产物的未知结构。

Conclusion: 大规模模拟预训练可以有效弥合模拟与真实数据之间的差距，实现对真实世界NMR光谱的鲁棒且可泛化的分子结构分析。

Abstract: Nuclear Magnetic Resonance (NMR) spectroscopy is a powerful tool for molecular structure analysis, and spectral artificial intelligence offers great potential for its rapid and automated interpretation. However, the scarcity of experimental NMR datasets has constrained deep learning in this domain to narrow, task-specific applications that lack broad generalization. Here, we introduce UltraNMR, a large-scale foundation model for NMR that leverages the intrinsic properties of NMR spectra to learn generalizable spectral representations. We collected 158 million paired simulated $^{1}$H and $^{13}$C NMR spectra to train UltraNMR, employing multiple domain-specific pre-training objectives. UltraNMR captures both intra-spectral and inter-spectral dependencies, enabling seamless simulation-to-real adaptation. We demonstrate that adapting UltraNMR to a range of molecular structure analysis tasks on experimental NMR spectra consistently yields state-of-the-art performance and clearly outperforms UltraNMR variants trained directly on downstream data without simulation pre-training. We also construct a large-scale NMR spectral vector library by encoding simulated NMR spectra using UltraNMR, covering 94 million unique molecules and enabling effective structure-aware retrieval. In real-world applications, UltraNMR facilitates the structural elucidation of two previously unknown natural products from Chinese herbal medicines recorded in the Chinese Pharmacopoeia. These results suggest that large-scale simulation pre-training can effectively bridge the simulation-to-real gap, enabling robust and generalizable molecular structure analysis of real-world NMR spectra.

</details>


### [88] [Controlling the phase behaviour of ultraconfined water via bilayer graphene stacking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21490)
*Yixuan Pu,Benjamin X. Shi,Pavan Ravindra,Chris Pickard,Angelos Michaelides,Venkat Kapil*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: AA和AB堆叠的双层石墨烯纳米毛细管中，尽管仅存在1.4 Å的横向偏移，却导致受限水的相图、熔化温度、质子传输和超离子行为发生显著变化，表明纳米受限水的行为不仅取决于几何限域，还高度依赖于壁面原子级排列。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统观点认为纳米受限水的异常行为主要源于几何限域和维度降低，但本文旨在探究限域壁的原子级结构变化（如堆叠方式）是否会对受限水性质产生决定性影响。

Method: 使用具有第一性原理精度的机器学习原子间势计算双层石墨烯纳米毛细管中受限水的密度-温度相图，对比AA和AB两种堆叠方式（仅1.4 Å横向偏移差异）。

Result: AA堆叠相比AB堆叠可稳定不同的冰多晶型、使熔化温度提升超过100 K、增强质子转移并改变超离子行为的起始条件；这些效应源于堆叠引起的氢键网络改变，与横向自由能景观和相邻O-O距离的修饰有关。

Conclusion: 即使限域壁的原子级细微变化也能定性重塑纳米受限水的物理和化学行为，对理解和控制埃尺度限域下的流体行为具有重要意义。

Abstract: Water confined within nanoscale capillaries exhibits phase behaviour and transport properties that differ substantially from bulk, and these effects are commonly interpreted as consequences of geometric confinement and reduced dimensionality. Here we show that confinement topology alone is insufficient to predict the behaviour of nanoconfined water. Using machine learning interatomic potentials with first-principles accuracy, we compute the density-temperature phase diagram of water confined within bilayer graphene nanocapillaries and compare AA and AB stacking arrangements, which differ only by a lateral shift of 1.4 Å. Despite this minimal structural change, AA stacking can stabilise different ice polymorphs, can increase the melting temperature by more than 100 K, can enhance proton transfer, and alters the onset of superionic behaviour relative to AB stacking. We trace these effects to stacking-induced changes in the hydrogen-bond network associated with modifications to the lateral free energy landscape and neighbouring O-O separations. Our results demonstrate that even subtle atomistic variations in the confining walls can qualitatively reshape the physical and chemical behaviour of nanoconfined water, with implications for the interpretation and control of fluids under angstrom-scale confinement.

</details>


### [89] [The impedance of a charged flat-plate electric double-layer capacitor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21980)
*Adrian L. Usler,David Fertig,Mathijs Janssen*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过有限元模拟修正的Poisson-Nernst-Planck方程，计算了平板EDL电容器的阻抗谱，发现偏压或阴阳离子扩散系数差异会导致Nyquist图中出现斜线，并将其归因于双极性盐扩散。文章反驳了此前关于斜线宽度代表EDL电阻、斜率代表时间尺度比的论断，并提出了两种等效电路模型（Warburg short和Warburg open），建立了Warburg因子与双极性扩散系数、本体电解质化学电容及EDL微分电荷效率的定量关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确理解平板电极EDL电容器的阻抗特性，澄清此前文献中对阻抗谱斜线区域物理含义的误解，建立更准确的等效电路模型和定量分析框架。

Method: 采用有限元方法数值求解修正的Poisson-Nernst-Planck方程，模拟不同偏压和离子扩散系数不对称条件下的阻抗谱；通过分析浓度扰动模式识别物理机制；提出两种等效电路模型进行定量拟合。

Result: 1) 确认偏压和非对称扩散均导致阻抗谱出现斜线，源于双极性盐扩散；2) 反驳了斜线宽度代表EDL电阻、斜率代表时间尺度比的先前观点；3) 提出针对非对称扩散（Warburg short）和偏压（Warburg open）的两种等效电路模型；4) 建立了Warburg因子与双极性扩散系数、化学电容、微分电荷效率的定量关系；5) 解释了斜线宽度在大偏压下的饱和、大离子填充分数下的消失以及封闭系统中总电容受离子数量限制的现象。

Conclusion: 本文为平板电极EDL电容器的阻抗分析提供了更准确的理论框架，纠正了此前文献中的错误解读，提出的等效电路模型和定量关系可用于指导实验数据分析。

Abstract: We calculate the impedance of a flat-plate electric double-layer (EDL) capacitor by means of Finite Element Method simulations of modified Poisson--Nernst--Planck equations. In Nyquist representation, the impedance spectra show a slanted line at intermediate frequencies if the capacitor is biased by a voltage, $U_\mathrm{bias}\neq 0$, or if the cation and anion diffusion coefficients differ, $D_-\neq D_+$. By inspecting the concentration perturbations in the relevant frequency range, we confirm that the slanted line is in both cases related to ambipolar salt diffusion. On the basis of our impedance data, we disprove two previously made claims: 1) that the width $R_\mathrm{sl}$ of the slanted-line region represents an EDL resistance; and 2) that the slope $k_\mathrm{sl}$ of the slanted line is a measure of the ratio of the diffusion and charging time scales, $τ_\mathrm{diff}/τ_\mathrm{c}$. For the quantitative analysis of flat-electrode EDL capacitor impedance, we propose instead two equivalent circuits, for the cases $D_-\neq D_+$ and $U_\mathrm{bias}\neq 0$. These two cases give rise to antisymmetric and symmetric salt perturbations, which are best described by a Warburg short and Warburg open element, respectively. From our circuit analysis, we obtain quantitative relations that link the Warburg prefactors to the ambipolar diffusion coefficient, the chemical capacitance of the bulk electrolyte, and the differential charge efficiency of the EDLs. We thus provide a theoretical framework that explains why the width of the slanted line saturates at large biases, why it vanishes for large ion packing fractions, and how the system's overall capacitance is limited by the finite amount of ions in a closed system.

</details>


### [90] [Reaction-Network-Level Discovery of Ammonia Synthesis Catalysts via Ten-Million-Scale Generative Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22926)
*Ruili Li,Rui Qi,Shuoqi Zhang,Qingli Tang,Qingqing Mao,Ritankar Das,Beien Zhu,Yi Gao*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于千万级生成式探索的反应网络级催化剂发现框架，通过耦合吸附质特异性生成Transformer和高通量机器学习势，系统映射四种关键中间体(N*、NH*、NNH*、HNNH*)的结构-性能景观，从约1500万种配置中筛选出279种高潜力催化材料，成功恢复了传统Fe/Ru基催化剂并发现了Fe-V（解离路径）和Al-Pd-Zr（缔合路径）等新家族。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 氨合成催化剂发现本质上是一个反应网络问题，因为催化性能由多个中间体在解离和缔合竞争路径上的协同兼容性决定，而非单一吸附中间体。然而，在超大规模化学空间中导航多中间体约束条件仍是传统筛选流程的瓶颈。

Method: 将吸附质特异性生成Transformer与高通量机器学习势耦合，在千万级生成尺度下系统映射四种关键中间体的结构-性能景观；通过尺度依赖性重叠分析揭示传统10^5-10^6尺度下的欠采样问题；对约1500万种构型进行结构压缩和机器学习势预测。

Result: 从约1500万种构型中识别出279种高潜力目标材料；稀疏兼容性空间成功恢复传统Fe基和Ru基催化剂，并发现此前未探索的催化剂家族；DFT计算验证了Fe-V通过显著降低初始N2解离势垒作为解离路径候选，Al-Pd-Zr通过高效稳定缔合中间体作为缔合路径候选。

Conclusion: 多中间体反应网络兼容性可作为从百万级生成化学空间中发现先进催化剂的稳健判据，千万级探索规模对于充分采样四中间体兼容空间至关重要。

Abstract: Catalyst discovery for ammonia synthesis is inherently a reaction-network challenge because catalytic performance is governed not by a single adsorbed intermediate, but by a surface's orchestrated compatibility with multiple distinct intermediates across competing dissociative and associative pathways. However, navigating ultra-large chemical spaces under such multi-intermediate constraints remains a formidable bottleneck for conventional screening workflows. Here, we report a reaction-network-level catalyst discovery framework driven by ten-million-scale generative exploration. By coupling adsorbate-specific generative Transformers with high-throughput machine learning potentials, we systematically map the structure-property landscapes of four critical intermediates (N*, NH*, NNH*, and HNNH*). Scale-dependent overlap analysis shows that the full four-intermediate compatibility space remains strongly under-sampled at conventional 105-106 generative scales, emerging exclusively under ten-million-scale exploration. By generating approximately 15 million configurations per adsorbate, followed by structural compression and machine-learning-potential predictions, we identified 279 highly potential target materials. This sparse compatibility space successfully recovers traditional Fe- and Ru-based motifs while uncovering previously unexplored catalyst families. Representative DFT calculations validate pathway-dependent mechanisms: Fe-V emerges as a dissociative-pathway lead by significantly lowering the initial N2 dissociation barrier, whereas Al-Pd-Zr efficiently stabilizes associative intermediates as an associative-pathway lead. These findings establish multi-intermediate reaction-network compatibility as a robust criterion for discovering advanced catalysts from multi-million generative chemical spaces.

</details>


### [91] [Local and Charge-Transfer Excitation of Pentacene-Buckminsterfullerene complexes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.23084)
*Ala Aldin M. Hani M. Darghouth,Omar A. Shareef,Firas A. AL-Lolage,Mark E. Casida*

Main category: physics.chem-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了并五苯-巴克敏斯特富勒烯有机太阳能电池四种不同界面构型模型中的电荷转移态、并五苯局域激发态和富勒烯局域激发态。通过CAM-B3LYP、B3LYP和DFTB三种方法对比发现：CAM-B3LYP（无需最优调参）计算的电荷转移态能量与OPT-wB97XD结果非常接近，而B3LYP和DFTB均低估约1 eV，无法正确描述电荷转移能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确描述有机太阳能电池中给体-受体界面间的电荷转移态能量对于理解其工作机制和优化器件性能至关重要，但不同理论方法在描述电荷转移态时存在显著差异，需要系统评估各种泛函和方法的适用性。

Method: 采用四种不同界面构型的并五苯-富勒烯模型，分别使用CAM-B3LYP（长程修正杂化泛函）、B3LYP（传统杂化泛函）和DFTB（密度泛函紧束缚）方法，结合含时密度泛函理论（TD-DFT）计算电荷转移态、给体局域激发态和受体局域激发态的能量，并与最优调参长程修正杂化泛函（OPT-wB97XD）结果进行对比。

Result: CAM-B3LYP（未进行最优调参）得到的电荷转移态能量与OPT-wB97XD结果非常接近；B3LYP和DFTB对所有模型均低估了约1 eV的电荷转移能，无法正确描述电荷转移态能量。

Conclusion: CAM-B3LYP泛函无需额外最优调参即可可靠描述并五苯-富勒烯体系的电荷转移态能量，而B3LYP和DFTB方法在该类体系中存在系统性低估，不适用于电荷转移能的准确计算。

Abstract: The charge transfer state, the local excited state on pentacene, and the local excited state on buckminsterfullerene have been studied for four models of the pentacene-buckminsterfullerene organic solar cell. These models have different interface configurations between the donor and the acceptor. The study has been done by using different functionals and time-dependent density functional theory-namely, the long range-separated hybrid with coulomb-attenuating method approach (CAM-B3LYP functional), the popular B3LYP (Becke, three-parameter, Lee- Yang-Parr) exchange-correlation functional, and the density functional tight binding (DFTB) method. The charge transfer state energy obtained by using CAM-B3LYP without optimally tuned range-separated hybrid parameters are very close to those obtained by using a many-body dispersion-corrected, optimally tuned range-separated hybrid functional (OPT-wB97XD). Both the B3LYP functional and the DFTB method fail to describe correctly the charge transfer energy for all models. Each of them is underestimating around 1 eV compared with range-separated hybrid functional.

</details>

### [92] [PEAR: Permutation-Equivariant Adaptive Routing Multi-Agent Debate](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20621)
*Yang Feng,Ziwei Xu,Xia Hu,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PEAR是一种置换等变自适应路由多智能体辩论协议，通过动态重新配置通信角色和稀疏拓扑，防止固定偏见，在四个推理基准和六个LLM骨干上显著优于现有辩论方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 固定拓扑的多智能体辩论存在持续性位置偏见、放大不可靠智能体以及对角色分配高度敏感等问题，需要一种能动态调整通信结构的方案来提升LLM推理可靠性。

Method: 提出置换等变自适应路由多智能体辩论（PEAR），在连续辩论轮次中动态重新配置智能体的通信角色和稀疏拓扑结构，基于演化中的智能体状态战略性地切换角色分配，并理论上证明其为等变稀疏路由器。

Result: 在四个推理基准和六个不同LLM骨干上的全面实验表明，PEAR显著提升了平均准确率，超越了最强辩论基线方法。

Conclusion: PEAR通过动态角色切换和稀疏拓扑优化，有效解决了固定拓扑辩论中的偏见问题，提升了多智能体辩论的鲁棒性和性能。

Abstract: Multi-agent debate improves the reliability of large language models (LLMs) through iterative peer critiques. However, fixed topologies often introduce persistent positional biases, amplify unreliable agents, and cause high sensitivity to role assignments. We introduce \textit{Permutation-Equivariant Adaptive Routing Multi-Agent Debate (PEAR)}, an inference-time protocol that dynamically reconfigures communication roles and sparse topologies across consecutive debate rounds. By strategically switching agent-to-role assignments based on evolving agent states, PEAR prevents any agent from permanently occupying a privileged network position or distributes influence more evenly across the debate. We theoretically characterize PEAR as an equivariant sparse router: it preserves accuracy under agent relabeling while reducing routing complexity and improving generalization. Comprehensive empirical evaluations across four reasoning benchmarks and six diverse LLM backbones demonstrate PEAR significantly improves average accuracy over the strongest debate baselines. The code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/PEAR.

</details>


### [93] [In LLM Reasoning, there is Irrationality on top of Value Misalignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20624)
*Kejiang Qian,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出“理性价值风险”（rational value risk）概念，形式化描述了即使经过良好对齐训练的LLM在推理时仍可能无法最大化对齐价值的现象。通过分解风险估计误差为三个来源（有限候选、有限提示、不完美验证器），并在多个模型和基准上验证了该风险的普遍性、对齐无法完全消除、对推理策略敏感以及长推理收益递减等发现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对齐方法（如RLHF）主要关注训练阶段的价值对齐，但忽略了模型在推理部署时即使已对齐仍可能偏离理性决策。作者旨在形式化并量化这一被忽视的“训练-推理对齐差距”。

Method: 形式化定义理性价值风险为部署推理策略与理性策略（最大化期望效用的最陡方向响应）之间的效用差异；将风险估计误差分解为有限候选、有限提示和不完美验证器三个分量；在Llama-3.1、Qwen-2.5、Tülu-3（7B-72B）、GPT-5.2、GPT-5.5、DeepSeek-V4等模型上，使用UltraFeedback、AlpacaEval、GSM8K、MATH、HumanEval、MathArena等基准进行实验验证。

Result: (1) 理性价值风险普遍存在；(2) 价值对齐可以降低但无法完全消除该风险；(3) 该风险对推理时的推理策略高度敏感；(4) 更长的推理链能提升理性程度，但存在边际收益递减效应。

Conclusion: LLM在训练阶段的价值对齐不足以保证推理时实现理性决策，需要关注推理策略的优化来弥合理性与对齐之间的差距。

Abstract: Significant progress has been made in aligning LLMs with target value functions. We argue that, even when an LLM has been well aligned in (post-)training, it may still fail to maximise the aligned value in reasoning.
