We mathematically formalise this gap as rational value risk: the utility discrepancy between a model's deployed reasoning strategy and its rational counterpart, which is defined to be the responses that maximise expected utility in the steepest direction. The estimation error of rational value risk is further decomposed into three components from finite candidates, finite prompts, and imperfect verifiers. Extensive experiments are conducted, covering models Llama-3.1, Qwen-2.5, T{\"}ulu-3 families (7B-72B), GPT-5.2, GPT-5.5, and DeepSeek-V4, and benchmarks UltraFeedback, AlpacaEval, GSM8K, MATH, HumanEval, and MathArena. The results validate that (1) rational value risk is widespread; (2) value alignment can reduce, but cannot eliminate, it; (3) the risk is highly sensitive to inference-time reasoning strategy; and (4) longer reasoning improves rationality with diminishing returns. The code is at https://github.com/EVIEHub/LLM-Rationality.

</details>


### [94] [AlphaMemo: Structured Search-Process Memory for Self-Evolving Alpha Mining Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20625)
*Hang Yu,Zifan Zheng,Jeff Z. Pan,Tongliang Liu,Zhiyong Wang,Fengxiang He*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出AlphaMemo，一种具有结构化搜索-过程记忆的自进化因子挖掘智能体，通过记录可复用的编辑模式证据、置信门控残差记忆和非对称否决控制，提升因子挖掘的样本外表现和发现效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体在因子挖掘中面临组合搜索空间巨大、噪声非平稳反馈、冗余发现以及过度拟合风险等挑战，现有方法仅记忆最终因子或完整轨迹，缺乏可复用证据的结构化存储。

Method: 1) 从抽象语法树(AST)差异中提取编辑模式(motifs)；2) 在搜索账本先验之上构建置信门控残差记忆；3) 使用非对称否决控制抑制高置信度的失败模式。

Result: 在CSI 500和S&P 500上的实验表明，AlphaMemo提升了样本外表现和固定预算下的发现效率，消融实验验证了残差学习、置信门控、AST差异模式和否决记忆各自的有效性。

Conclusion: 结构化搜索-过程记忆机制能够有效提升LLM因子挖掘智能体的鲁棒性和效率，代码已开源。

Abstract: LLM agents are promising for alpha mining via combining financial priors, symbolic reasoning, executable factor generation, and feedback-driven refinement. Yet, they face a combinatorial search space, noisy non-stationary feedback, redundant discoveries, and overfitting risks from naively reusing past successes. To address these challenges, we propose AlphaMemo, a self-evolving alpha mining agent with Structured Search-Process Memory. Rather than memorizing only final factors or full trajectories, AlphaMemo records reusable evidence about which edit motifs work or fail under specific parent-factor contexts. It extracts motifs from Abstract Syntax Tree (AST) differences, applies confidence-gated residual memory on top of a search-ledger prior, and uses asymmetric veto control to suppress high-confidence failure patterns. Experiments on CSI 500 and S\&P 500 show improved out-of-sample performance and fixed-budget discovery efficiency, with ablations validating the roles of residual learning, confidence gating, AST-diff motifs, and veto memory. Code is at https://github.com/jarrettyu/AlphaMemo.

</details>


### [95] [Harnessing Agent Skills: Architectural Patterns and a Reference Architecture for Skill-Mediated LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20631)
*Boming Xia,Liming Zhu,Zhenchang Xing,Qinghua Lu,Dino Sejdinovic,Xiwei Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了LLM智能体中技能工件（skill artefacts）向“运行中技能”（skill-in-use）转化的架构责任，提出了包含十个经验驱动模式（五个核心、五个支撑）的分层参考架构（供应链、中介、执行控制、证据与反馈），并通过8个系统跨实例化验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体技能作为可复用的行为知识以静态工件形式存在，但在实际运行时需要与上下文、权限约束绑定，并经过随机智能体解释后产生运行证据。这一从静态到动态的转化过程缺乏系统性的架构责任分析，现有研究未能清晰界定技能工件与技能运行之间的架构边界与职责。

Method: 采用经验驱动的方法，通过实证观察提炼出十个架构模式（五个核心模式处理技能激活与绑定，五个支撑模式处理证据与反馈），并将其综合为四层参考架构（供应链层、中介层、执行控制层、证据与反馈层），最后通过8个代表性系统进行跨实例化评估。

Result: 提出了一个包含四层责任（供应链、中介、执行控制、证据与反馈）的参考架构，以及十个经验驱动的架构模式。通过8个系统的跨实例化验证了该架构的适用性和解释力，表明其能够有效描述不同智能体系统中技能驾驭责任的分布与实现方式。

Conclusion: 本文提出的模式与参考架构为分析智能体系统中的技能驾驭责任提供了统一的词汇表和诊断框架，有助于系统设计者理解、比较和改进不同系统中从技能工件到技能运行转化的架构决策。跨实例化评估验证了该架构的通用性和实用性。

Abstract: Agent skills externalise reusable agent-facing behavioural knowledge and guidance as persistent artefacts that can be discovered, activated, and interpreted by LLM agents. Although a skill artefact is static at rest, its architectural responsibilities arise in use, when the artefact is selected for a run, bound to context and authority constraints, interpreted by a stochastic agent, and recorded as run evidence. We call this run-specific relation skill-in-use. This paper studies agent skill harnessing: the architectural responsibilities that govern the transition from skill artefacts to skill-in-use, bound the executable consequences associated with skill-in-use, and capture evidence for attribution, verification, repair, and evolution. This paper provides a catalogue of ten empirically grounded architectural patterns (five core, five supporting) for skill harnessing and synthesises them into a reference architecture with four responsibility layers: Supply Chain, Mediation, Execution Control, and Evidence & Feedback. We evaluate the architecture through cross-instantiation across 8 selected systems. The resulting patterns and reference architecture provide a vocabulary and diagnostic frame for analysing skill-harnessing responsibilities across agent systems.

</details>


### [96] [An LLM-Explainable DRL Framework for Passenger-Directed Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20640)
*Ouided Braoui,Meriem Bouali,Nadir Farhi*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合深度强化学习（DRL）和大型语言模型（LLM）的框架，DRL负责自适应驾驶控制，LLM负责生成可解释性说明，以提升自动驾驶车辆的透明度和乘客信任。实验表明，该框架在安全性、适应性和可解释性之间取得了平衡，尤其在请求被延迟或安全约束覆盖时效果最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶车辆决策过程缺乏透明度，导致公众信任度低。现有方法未有效解决如何让乘客理解并信任车辆行为的问题。

Method: 使用Dueling Double Deep Q-Network训练DRL智能体实现快、舒适、停止三种驾驶模式；引入LLM模块解释乘客请求、判断何时需要解释，并生成简洁的安全导向说明。

Result: DRL智能体实现了稳定学习、安全合规行驶以及单次行程中模式间可靠切换；LLM模块在请求被延迟或安全约束覆盖时最有效。

Conclusion: 该框架作为RL决策与LLM可解释性集成的概念验证，成功平衡了安全性、适应性和可解释性，为提升自动驾驶信任度提供了可行方向。

Abstract: Autonomous vehicles offer the potential for safer and more efficient mobility, yet public trust remains limited due to the lack of transparency in their decision-making. This work addresses this issue by combining deep reinforcement learning (DRL) for adaptive driving control with large language model (LLM)-based explainability modules designed to communicate agent behavior to passengers. DRL agents were trained in simulation using a Dueling Double Deep Q-Network to follow distinct driving requests: \textit{fast}, \textit{comfort}, and \textit{stop}. They demonstrated stable learning, safe compliance with traffic rules, and reliable switching between modes within a single trip. In parallel, LLM modules were introduced to interpret passenger requests, determine when explanations were needed, and generate concise, safety-oriented justifications. Results show that this framework, serving as a proof of concept for integrating RL decision-making and LLMs, balances safety, adaptability, and explainability, and is most effective when requests are delayed or overridden due to safety constraints.

</details>


### [97] [ARIA: A Causal-Aware Framework for Rescuing LLM Reasoning in Trustworthy Materials Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22375)
*Yi Cao,Liaoyaqi Wang,Jieneng Chen,Benjamin Van Durme,Alan Yuille,Paulette Clancy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出ARIA框架，通过三级因果推理级联（直接因果推理、物理类比迁移、参数化回退）缓解大语言模型在材料发现中的"上下文隧道效应"（过度锚定局部证据而抑制全局物理推理），在含2,839条PSP关系的知识图谱上验证了其提升前向预测与逆向设计性能，并产生可审计的因果轨迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式模型在材料发现中常违反物理因果性，LLM增强知识图谱时会出现"上下文隧道效应"——模型过度锚定检索到的狭窄证据，抑制了全局物理推理能力。

Method: 提出ARIA框架，采用三级因果推理级联：(i) 当完整PSP证据链可用时进行直接因果推理；(ii) 针对稀疏或新材料系统进行物理类比迁移；(iii) 外部证据不完备时显式参数化回退。构建含2,839条PSP关系的知识图谱，在二维材料上评估前向预测和逆向设计任务。

Result: ARIA缓解了上下文隧道效应，性能优于未增强和朴素KG增强基线，在线文献搜索进行证据增强时获得进一步提升，且产生可审计的因果轨迹。

Conclusion: ARIA实现了物理上有根据且可信赖的AI辅助材料发现，为因果感知的LLM应用提供了有效框架。

Abstract: Generative models have revolutionized the process of materials discovery, yet they often fail to satisfy underlying physical causality. Through an analysis of Large Language Models (LLMs) augmented with knowledge graphs derived from current literature, we uncover a phenomenon termed contextual tunneling, where models "over-anchor" on narrow, retrieved evidence while suppressing global physical reasoning. To address this problem, we introduce ARIA, a causal-aware framework that conditions knowledge use on mechanistic completeness. ARIA routes each query through a three-tier cascade: (i) direct causal reasoning when complete evidence chains of Process-Structure-Property (PSP) are available, (ii) physics-informed analogical transfer for sparse or novel material systems, and (iii) explicit parametric fallback when external evidence is incomplete. As a proof of concept, we construct a Knowledge Graph (KG) containing 2,839 extracted PSP relations from peer-reviewed articles in the materials literature and evaluate ARIA on forward prediction and inverse design tasks for two-dimensional (2D) materials. ARIA mitigates contextual tunneling, improves over unaugmented and naive KG-augmented baselines, and provides further gains when an online literature search is used for evidence enrichment. Crucially, ARIA produces auditable causal traces, enabling physically grounded and trustworthy AI-assisted materials discovery.

</details>


### [98] [Hypothesis-Disciplined Multi-Agent Automated Formalization of Asymptotic Statistical Theory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20642)
*Tingzhou Wei,Zeyu Zheng,Ethan X. Fang,Junwei Lu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种由多智能体协作的假设驱动型Lean 4形式化验证管线，用于处理渐近统计理论中复杂的收敛性、渐近展开和泛函分析等内容的自动化形式化，并通过审计机制确保源文本忠实性和公理清洁性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 渐近统计理论中收敛性陈述、渐近展开、泛函分析与现有Lean 4形式化基础设施之间存在巨大差距，该领域是AI辅助形式化的挑战性领域。

Method: 提出了一种假设驱动的Lean 4形式化管线，包含一个管理者（Manager）和七个专业角色（规划、骨架搭建、Mathlib侦察、证明构建、集成、独立审阅、审计）；核心方法是审计员（Auditor）执行的假设驱动审计，确保每个定理假设和概念层字段必须锚定于源数学散文、被证明为Lean编码适配器、标记为源隐含或被拒绝为不受支持的强化。

Result: 成功系统化形式化了渐近统计理论，包括参数和半参数模型的渐近分布与效率结果，生成了公理清洁、源忠实的形式化开发，已通过Lean检查和人工审计，代码开源。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了假设驱动的多智能体形式化管线在处理复杂数学理论形式化中的有效性，实现了定理无关的基础设施与定理特定组件的分离组织，为AI辅助形式化提供了可复用的方法论框架。

Abstract: Asymptotic statistical theory is a challenging domain for AI-assisted formalization: its central results mix convergence statements, asymptotic expansions, functional analysis, and regularity conditions that have a large gap from existing infrastructure in Lean 4 formalization. To address these challenges, we propose a hypothesis-disciplined Lean 4 formalization pipeline built from multiple agents: a manager that coordinates seven specialist roles for proof planning, skeleton scaffolding, Mathlib reconnaissance, proof construction, integration, independent review, and audit. The main methodological discipline is the hypothesis-disciplined audit, implemented by the Auditor agent: every main-theorem hypothesis and concept-layer field must be anchored in the source mathematical prose, justified as a Lean encoding adapter, marked as source-implied, or rejected as an unsupported strengthening. Using this workflow, we build a systematic formalization of asymptotic statistical theory, especially the parametric and semi-parametric models' asymptotic distribution and efficiency results. The resulting Lean development is axiom-clean and source-faithful, with Lean-checked and human-audited proofs of core parametric and semi-parametric theorems organized so that theorem-agnostic infrastructure and statistical concept definitions are separated from theorem-specific assembly. The formalization results are available at https://github.com/junwei-lu/Lean-Asymptotic-Statistical-Theory.

</details>


### [99] [MetaPS: Adaptive Programmatic Strategy Selection for Market Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22385)
*Jiaxiang Chen,Aotian Luo,Zhouyi Zheng,Weiyi Huang,Chi Zhang,Zenglin Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出MetaPS框架，通过模拟市场环境引导大语言模型从预定义的程序化策略库中选择最优策略，而不是直接生成市场动作。实验表明该方法在多个模型规模上优于固定策略、直接决策和API-based LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单一市场策略无法在所有市场条件下获胜，而直接让大语言模型生成市场动作缺乏可解释性和可靠性。作者希望利用市场模拟提供可扩展的监督信号，学习自适应、可解释且可执行的策略选择。

Method: MetaPS框架包含三个阶段：(1) 在模拟/回测市场中展开候选策略；(2) 识别特定状态下能带来更好未来结果的策略-状态对，转化为监督微调数据；(3) 推理时仅观察当前市场状态和候选策略上下文，选择合适策略程序执行。

Result: 在多股票交易和商品交换沙盒实验中，MetaPS在0.8B到9B参数模型规模上均一致提升；优于固定策略、直接决策和API-based LLM agent基线；紧凑微调模型甚至超越更强的API模型。

Conclusion: 市场模拟可以为学习自适应、可解释且可执行的策略选择提供可扩展的定向监督，证明模拟引导的策略选择框架具有实用价值。

Abstract: No single market strategy always wins: momentum, mean reversion, risk control,and event-driven rules can each succeed or fail as market conditions change.Rather than asking large language models to directly generate market actions,we study an executable decision paradigm where an agent selects from a library of programmatic strategies, each implemented as a code module mapping market observations to actions.We propose \textbf{MetaPS}, a simulation-guided framework for adaptive programmatic strategy selection. MetaPS rolls out candidate strategies in simulated or backtested markets, identifies states where particular strategies lead to better future outcomes, and converts these state--strategy pairs into supervised fine-tuning data. During inference, the simulator is no longer queried: MetaPS observes only the current market state and candidate strategy context, selects a suitable strategy program, and the selected program produces the final action. Experiments on multi-stock trading and a controlled goods-exchange sandbox show that MetaPS consistently improves across model scales from 0.8B to 9B parameters. It outperforms fixed-strategy baselines, direct decision-making agents, and prompted API-based LLM agents; in several settings, compact fine-tuned models even surpass stronger API models. These results demonstrate that market simulations can provide scalable and targeted supervision for learning adaptive, interpretable, and executable strategy selection.

</details>


### [100] [SPARC: A Multi-Agent System for Electrical Circuit Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20643)
*Mushtari Sadia,Zhenning Yang,Umme Habiba Lamia,Nishat Shawrin,Ang Chen,Amrita Roy Chowdhury*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SPARC是一个多智能体系统，通过可执行的基于物理的模拟来回答电路图问题，相比基线方法实现了83%的准确率和高达58%的绝对性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电路图问答任务需要复杂的数学推理，这对多模态大语言模型仍然具有挑战性。

Method: 提出SPARC，一个多智能体系统，利用LLM智能体合成、执行和分析模拟程序，将推理 grounding 在可执行的基于物理的模拟中。

Result: SPARC达到83%的准确率，相比基线方法获得高达58%的绝对改进，并支持系统性的错误诊断。

Conclusion: 通过将推理过程锚定在可执行的物理模拟中，SPARC显著提高了电路图问答任务的准确性和可靠性。

Abstract: Electrical circuit diagram QA tasks require complex mathematical reasoning, which remains challenging for multimodal LLMs. We present SPARC, a multi-agent system that answers questions over circuit diagrams by grounding reasoning in executable physics-based simulations. SPARC uses LLM agents to synthesize, execute, and analyze simulation programs, improving accuracy and reliability by design. It achieves 83% accuracy, with up to a 58% absolute improvement over baselines, while enabling systematic error diagnosis.

</details>


### [101] [Skill Coverage: A Test Adequacy Metric for Agent Skills](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20659)
*Boyin Tan,Xiaowei Huang,Youcheng Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出技能覆盖率（skill coverage）作为衡量LLM智能代理技能测试充分性的指标，发现现有基准测试中仅覆盖39.90-43.98%的技能行为约束，表明任务成功并不等同于技能工件的充分测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估主要关注任务层面的结果，但任务成功无法揭示技能的哪些部分已被执行、哪些尚未测试，缺乏对技能工件本身测试充分性的度量。

Method: 将技能工件视为测试对象，从技能文档中提取可观察的行为约束，通过二元覆盖/未覆盖判断来评估智能代理轨迹是否为每个约束提供了充分的观察证据。

Result: 对SkillsBench的应用显示，现有基准执行仅覆盖39.90-43.98%的技能行为约束，表明大量技能指导未被测试。

Conclusion: 成功的任务完成并不等同于对技能工件的充分测试，技能覆盖率可作为衡量智能代理技能测试全面性的重要指标。

Abstract: Agent skills encode reusable procedural knowledge that guides large language model agents across tasks and execution contexts. Existing evaluations primarily assess skills through task level outcomes, yet task success alone does not reveal which parts of a skill have been exercised or which remain untested. We introduce skill coverage, a test adequacy metric that treats the skill artifact as the object under test. Our approach extracts observable skill behavior constraints from skill documents and measures whether an agent trajectory provides sufficient evidence to exercise and evaluate each constraint. Skill coverage uses a binary cover or not cover judgment, which reports whether a documented behavior has been exercised with sufficient observable evidence, without assigning an additional outcome label to the behavior. Applying skill coverage to SkillsBench reveals that existing benchmark executions cover only 39.90 to 43.98% of skill behavior constraints, suggesting that current benchmark tasks leave large portions of documented skill guidance untested. These findings show that successful task completion does not imply adequate testing of the skill artifact, highlighting skill coverage as a measure of how thoroughly agent skills are tested.

</details>


### [102] [From Knowing to Acting: Benchmarking Self-Awareness Capability of LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20661)
*Yifan Li,Shengbin Yue,Boyu Feng,Jinhu Qi,Bo Ke,Zixing Song,Hongru Wang,Zhongyu Wei,Irwin King*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出KAPRO框架和KAware数据集，用于评估LLM智能体的自我意识能力——即判断问题需要外部工具还是内部参数知识来解决。实验发现自我意识与任务成功率强相关，开源和指令跟随模型更易过度使用工具，而专有和推理模型具有更可靠的认知门控能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准主要评估执行成功率，忽略了智能体的自我意识能力——即判断问题是否需要外部资源还是可以依靠内部参数知识解决的能力。

Method: 提出KAPRO (Knowing-Acting Quadrant PRObe) 框架，将智能体的元认知判断（Knowing）与自发执行（Acting）解耦，评估认知-行为对齐度。同时构建KAware数据集，将任务严格划分为外部、内部和混合子空间，系统性地探测知识边界。

Result: 自我意识与任务成功率强相关，但在内部能力场景下急剧下降。开源和指令跟随模型因浅层模式匹配表现出更强的工具过度使用，而专有和推理模型展现出更可靠的认知门控能力。

Conclusion: 自我意识能力是影响LLM智能体任务成功的关键因素，不同模型家族在该能力上存在显著差异，需要更多关注智能体的认知-行为对齐。

Abstract: The integration of external tools has transitioned LLM agents from passive responders to autonomous systems. However, current benchmarks prioritize execution success, neglecting self-awareness capability, the ability to discern whether a problem requires necessary external resources or can be solved via internal parametric knowledge. To address this, we introduce KAPRO (Knowing-Acting Quadrant PRObe), a framework that evaluates cognitive-behavioral alignment by decoupling an agent's metacognitive judgment (Knowing) from its spontaneous execution (Acting). We further construct KAware, a dataset rigorously partitioning tasks into external, internal, and hybrid subspaces to systematically probe these epistemic boundaries. Extensive experiments across diverse agent architectures show that self-awareness capability is strongly correlated with task success but degrades sharply in internal-capability settings. Moreover, open-source and instruction-following models exhibit stronger tool overuse due to shallow pattern matching, while proprietary and reasoning-oriented models demonstrate more reliable cognitive gating. Benchmark and codes are available at https://github.com/AI-Santiago/KAware.

</details>


### [103] [A Quantum-Assisted Agentic Distributed Artificial Intelligence Framework for Deadline-Bounded Orchestration of Hybrid Renewable Microgrids](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20667)
*Iacovos I. Ioannou,Saher Javaid,Minella Bezha,Yasuo Tan,Naoto Nagaoka,Vasos Vassiliou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种量子辅助的分布式人工智能（DAI）框架，用于微电网的实时调度。该框架将每个控制时隙的调度问题建模为二次无约束二元优化（QUBO）问题，并通过量子、量子启发式及经典求解器组合求解。通过信念-愿望-意图扩展（BDIx）智能体进行求解器选择，配合信念塑造的储能估值机制，实现了零截止时间违规、精确最优调度（日成本146.24欧元）、97.83%可再生能源利用率及零未供应能量；去激活储能机制后成本上升4.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微电网实时协调调度面临组合优化与严格截止时间约束的双重挑战，传统方法难以同时满足求解质量与实时性要求。

Method: 提出量子辅助DAI框架，每个时隙的调度问题由BDIx智能体建模为QUBO问题，通过组合量子（QAOA）、量子启发式及经典求解器求解。协调智能体基于学习的延迟信念选择求解器，并引入信念塑造的储能估值机制将跨期信息注入单时隙优化。

Result: 24小时仿真中，零截止时间违规，每时隙精确最优调度，日成本146.24欧元（等于精确下界），可再生能源利用率97.83%，零未供应能量。去激活储能机制后成本升至152.75欧元（增加4.5%）。

Conclusion: 所提框架在实时性约束下实现了微电网调度的精确最优解，信念塑造的储能估值机制有效降低了运行成本。

Abstract: The real-time orchestration of microgrids that combine fluctuating renewable sources, dispatchable units, storage and curtailable consumers requires the repeated solution of combinatorial dispatch and coalition formation problems under hard control deadlines. In this paper, a quantum-assisted agentic distributed artificial intelligence (DAI) framework is proposed in which the dispatch problem of each control slot is formulated as a quadratic unconstrained binary optimization (QUBO) problem by Belief-Desire-Intention extended (BDIx) agents and is solved by a portfolio of quantum, quantum-inspired and classical solvers. Solver selection is elevated to a first-class agentic deliberation action of the coordinator agent. Learned beliefs about solver latencies are maintained and the solver intention that is expected to satisfy the prevailing deliberation deadline is committed in each slot. In addition, a belief-shaped storage valuation mechanism is introduced through which the storage agent prices its energy at a discounted future-peak value, injecting intertemporal information into the otherwise myopic per-slot optimization. The framework is evaluated on a 24-hour simulation of a grid-connected microgrid with photovoltaic, wind, battery, genset and demand-response assets, with the Quantum Approximate Optimization Algorithm (QAOA) executed by statevector simulation and benchmarked per slot against tabu search, simulated annealing, binary particle swarm optimization, greedy descent and exhaustive enumeration. Zero deliberation deadlines are missed, the committed dispatch attains the exact optimum on every slot and the realized daily cost of 146.24 EUR equals the exact lower bound, with 97.83 percent renewable utilization and zero unserved energy. When the storage valuation mechanism is deactivated, the daily cost is increased to 152.75 EUR, a 4.5 percent increase.

</details>


### [104] [Agent Behavior Mining: Generative AI Agent Governance in Business Processes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20669)
*Hoang Vu,Maximilian Körner,Adrian Rebmann,Gabriel Kevorkian,Michael Perscheid,Gregor Berg,Timotheus Kampik*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为"Agent行为挖掘"的治理能力，通过将生成式AI代理的推理轨迹、工具使用和Token成本等细粒度活动转化为标准化流程日志，使代理决策变得可观察和可追溯。研究在18位行业从业者的探索性评估中验证了该方法对政策偏差检测和操作变异性量化的实用性，结果显示行为透明度被视为建立信任的前提条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 组织在部署生成式AI代理以自动化业务流程时面临一个治理困境：这些代理虽然能提升操作灵活性，但其非确定性本质挑战了业务流程管理（BPM）所追求的控制与标准化，形成了所谓的"不可见自主性风险"。

Method: (1) 提出事件数据模型，将代理的推理轨迹、工具使用和Token成本等细粒度活动转换为标准化流程日志；(2) 在多代理订单到现金（order-to-cash）实现中实例化该数据模型，展示流程管理者如何利用代理日志检测策略偏差并量化操作变异性；(3) 通过18位行业从业者的探索性研究评估该方法的感知实用性。

Result: 从业者认为行为透明度是建立信任的必要条件，并将审查代理推理的能力视为下一代AI驱动业务流程的重要治理需求。

Conclusion: Agent行为挖掘能够使生成式AI代理的决策过程变得可观察和可追溯，解决了不可见自主性风险，为AI驱动业务流程的治理提供了可行的技术路径。

Abstract: As organizations increasingly deploy generative AI agents to automate business processes, they face a governance dilemma: although these agents can increase operational flexibility, their non-deterministic nature challenges the control and standardization that Business Process Management seeks to enforce. This paper addresses this \emph{invisible autonomy risk} by introducing \emph{Agent Behavior Mining}, a governance capability that enables the application of process mining techniques to render generative AI agent decision-making observable and traceable. We (1) improve the understanding of generative AI agent behavior through an event data model that translates granular agent activities -- including reasoning traces, tool usage, and token costs -- into standardized process logs; (2) instantiate the data model in a multi-agent order-to-cash implementation, demonstrating how process managers can leverage agent logs to detect policy deviations and quantify operational variability; and (3) evaluate the perceived practical utility of the approach in an exploratory study with 18 industry practitioners. The results indicate that practitioners view behavioral transparency as a prerequisite for trust and consider the ability to examine agent reasoning as an important governance requirement for the next generation of AI-driven business processes.

</details>


### [105] [Democratizing and accelerating AI-driven pathology research through agentic intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20677)
*Jiabo Ma,Cheng Jin,Yihui Wang,Hao Jiang,Ling Liang,Yingxue Xu,Junlin Hou,Zhengrui Guo,Zhengyu Zhang,Yifei Xia,Hongyi Wang,Fengtao Zhou,Zhe Xu,Huajun Zhou,Jiarui Ouyang,Qian Zeng,On Ki Tang,Eunhyang Park,Carolyn Glass,Ronald Cheong Kin Chan,Li Liang,Hao Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: PathLab是一个自主智能体框架，能将自然语言研究目标转化为可执行的、经过验证的计算病理学工作流。它通过可复用的方法学模块（数据预处理、模型开发、评估与解释）实现从科学意图到计算执行的自动化。在12个公开数据集、四类代表性任务（ROI分类、全切片图像分类、分割、生存预测）中均达到非劣效于专家实现的性能，并能主动拒绝不兼容的工作流规范。用户研究显示，PathLab大幅减少了生成可执行分析管线的时间，使无编程经验的领域专家也能独立完成计算病理学研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管基础模型推动了计算病理学的快速发展，但技术复杂性和编程要求限制了其广泛采用。现有方法要求用户具备编程专业知识才能设计和执行研究，形成了技术壁垒。

Method: PathLab采用自主智能体框架，通过结构化组合领域特定技能和工具，将自然语言研究目标转化为可执行的计算病理学工作流。工作流生成围绕可复用方法学模块（数据预处理、模型开发、评估与解释）组织，使研究在科学意图层面而非实现细节层面被指定。

Result: 在12个公开数据集、四类代表性任务（ROI分类、全切片图像分类、分割、生存预测）中，PathLab达到非劣效于专家实现的性能；能强制执行用户提示的语义有效性并主动拒绝不兼容工作流；用户研究中大幅缩短生成可执行分析管线的时间，使无编程经验的领域专家能独立完成研究。

Conclusion: PathLab建立了生物医学意图与计算执行之间的可靠接口，使计算病理学研究可在科学问题层面而非编程技术层面被设计。通过降低先进AI方法论的技术门槛，PathLab为计算病理学的更广泛民主化奠定了基础。

Abstract: Computational pathology has advanced rapidly with the emergence of foundation models, yet widespread adoption remains limited by substantial technical complexity and programming requirements. Here we present PathLab, an autonomous agentic framework that translates natural-language research objectives into executable and validated computational pathology workflows through the structured composition of domain-specific skills and tools. By organizing workflow generation around reusable methodological modules, including data preprocessing, model development, evaluation and interpretation, PathLab enables studies to be specified at the level of scientific intent rather than implementation details. We evaluated PathLab across 12 public datasets spanning four representative task families: region-of-interest classification, whole-slide image classification, segmentation and survival prediction. Across all task categories, PathLab achieved non-inferior performance relative to expert implementations, while consistently enforcing semantic validity of user prompts and proactively rejecting incompatible workflow specifications prior to execution. In controlled user studies, PathLab substantially reduced the time required to generate executable analytical pipelines and enabled domain experts without programming experience to independently design, execute and evaluate computational pathology studies. Together, these results establish PathLab as a reliable interface between biomedical intent and computational execution, enabling computational pathology studies to be designed at the level of scientific questions rather than programming expertise. By lowering technical barriers to advanced AI methodologies, PathLab provides a foundation for the broader democratization of computational pathology.

</details>


### [106] [From Question Answering to Task Completion: A Survey on Agent System and Harness Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20683)
*Jianyuan Guo,Zhiwei Hao,Chengcheng Wang,Cheng Fan,Tingzhang Luo,Hongguang Li,Ying Gao,Hefei Mei,Jiankun Peng,Rongjian Xu,Minjing Dong,Han Wu,Mengyu Zheng,Kai Han,Shiqi Wang,Chang Xu,Yunhe Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从模型-执行框架（model-harness）视角审视LLM智能体，将智能体分解为基础模型与执行框架的耦合，分析模型中心化scaling的局限性，梳理智能体工程的四个范式，并将执行框架拆解为观察、上下文、控制、动作、状态和验证六项运行时职责。文章认为智能体质量（成功率、效率、安全性和泛化能力）源于模型能力、运行时基础设施、任务结构和评估设计之间的交互，而非模型+工具的简单组合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着基于LLM的智能体系统从提示工程演进到工作流、上下文工程、框架工程以及智能体原生训练（共演化），智能体性能的瓶颈究竟在基础模型、执行框架还是二者的耦合上成为一个核心问题。作者希望建立统一的分析框架来系统审视这一问题。

Method: 采用模型-执行框架（model-harness）的二元视角，对智能体工程进行范式梳理（四个范式），将执行框架解构为六项运行时职责（观察、上下文、控制、动作、状态、验证），并基于此映射任务属性和领域压力到框架配置，综合评述基准测试与评估实践。

Result: 识别出运行时设计如何影响长程任务完成、效率和可靠性；揭示了模型-框架间的耦合证据；明确了价值感知评估、安全性、框架泛化以及模型-框架共演化等开放挑战。

Conclusion: 智能体质量并非单纯由模型能力决定，而是模型能力、运行时基础设施、任务结构和评估设计四者交互作用的涌现结果。不应将智能体视作带有辅助工具的模型。

Abstract: LLM-based agents mark a shift from passive question answering to active task completion: they perceive environments, invoke tools, maintain state, and act over extended horizons. As agent systems have evolved from prompt engineering to workflows and context engineering, harness engineering, and agent-native training with co-evolution, a central question has become increasingly important: where does the bottleneck in agent performance reside, in the foundation model, in the execution harness, or in the coupling between them? This survey examines LLM-based agents through a model-harness lens. We first clarify the functional definition of agents and the implementation view of an LLM-based agent as a foundation model coupled with an execution harness. We then analyze the limits of model-centric scaling, trace four paradigms of agent engineering, and decompose the execution harness into six coupled runtime responsibilities: observation, context, control, action, state, and verification. Using this decomposition, we map task properties and domain pressures to harness configurations, review benchmark and evaluation practices, and synthesize model-harness evidence on how runtime design affects long-horizon task completion, efficiency, and reliability. Finally, we identify open challenges in value-aware evaluation, safety, harness generalization, and model-harness co-evolution. Rather than treating agents as models with auxiliary tools, this survey argues that agent quality -- including success, efficiency, safety, and generalization -- emerges from the interaction between model capability, runtime infrastructure, task structure, and evaluation design. A collection of papers discussed in this survey is provided in https://github.com/ggjy/Awesome-Agent-Engineering.

</details>


### [107] [Simulated Customers Never Walk Away: Decision Fidelity of LLM User Simulators Measured Against Real Purchase Outcomes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20708)
*Liang Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 现有LLM用户模拟框架仅关注“沟通保真度”（模拟器说话像不像人），忽略了“决策保真度”——即模拟器能否复现真实用户在面临实际选择时的决策状态动态。本文基于2,790段真实销售对话（含793笔支付结果），发现模拟器存在系统性“脱离赤字”：能准确模拟购买者，但严重高估非购买者的参与度（抵抗行为减半、深思行为翻倍），且这一缺陷跨模型存在、难以通过简单指令修复。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM用户模拟的保真度评估只关注沟通层面（模拟器是否像人类一样说话），但忽略了更关键的问题：模拟器能否像真实用户一样做出决策，尤其是当真实用户的动机是内生的、潜在的、会衰减的。由于现有基准数据来自被分配任务的角色扮演参与者（动机外生），无法检验这一盲点。

Method: 使用2,790段真实生产环境中的LLM销售代理与真实客户对话（含793笔支付结果），采用教师强制探针协议（teacher-forced probe protocol），固定上下文和工具不变，比较模拟器与真实用户在决策状态动态上的差异。测试了不同模型家族（如DeepSeek）并尝试了简单指令修正。

Result: 模拟器存在系统性“脱离赤字”：对最终购买者的模拟几乎完美（深度偏差+0.09），但对最终非购买者的模拟严重失真（深度偏差+0.40），表现为抵抗表达减半（25.1%→13.5%）、深思行为翻倍（21.9%→40.1%），且不产生虚假购买。该缺陷跨模型存在（DeepSeek: d=0.41, p=0.002），简单指令修正效果有限（条件对比d仍为0.34, p=0.008）。

Conclusion: 现有LLM用户模拟框架在关键的非购买者群体上存在系统性偏差，会高估销售对话系统的漏斗进展。评估和训练销售/说服Agent时需引入决策保真度指标，否则最关键的“流失客户”群体被错误模拟为仍具参与度的潜在客户。

Abstract: LLM-as-user-simulation has become core infrastructure for conversational AI: agent benchmarks (tau-bench), training pipelines, and a growing body of fidelity studies all rely on LLMs role-playing the human side of dialogue. Existing frameworks measure communicative fidelity -- whether simulators talk like humans -- against ground truth from paid participants role-playing assigned goals. We argue this has a structural blind spot: when the goal is assigned, the user's willingness is exogenous, so no framework can test whether simulators make decisions like real users whose motivation is endogenous, latent, and decaying. We introduce decision fidelity -- whether a simulated population reproduces the decision-state dynamics of real users facing real, consequential choices -- and measure it on a unique testbed: 2,790 production conversations between an LLM sales agent and real customers, including 793 with verified payment outcomes. Using a teacher-forced probe protocol that holds context and instrument fixed, we find a systematic, outcome-correlated failure we call the disengagement deficit: simulators reproduce eventual buyers almost exactly (depth bias +0.09) but inflate eventual non-buyers toward the purchase frame (depth bias +0.40; d=0.38, p<0.001), halving expressed resistance (25.1% to 13.5%) and nearly doubling deliberation (21.9% to 40.1%) while fabricating no purchases. The deficit replicates across model families (DeepSeek: d=0.41, p=0.002) and resists the obvious fix: instructing the simulator that it may disengage cuts marginal bias five-fold but barely moves the outcome-conditioned contrast (d=0.34, p=0.008). Real non-buyers say "not now" and stop; simulated non-buyers ask about price. Evaluating or training sales and persuasion agents against such simulators overstates funnel progress exactly where it matters most -- the customers who walk away.

</details>


### [108] [FairTutor: Equity-Aware Pedagogical LLM Routing for Budget-Constrained AI Tutoring](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20713)
*Qingyang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出FairTutor框架，通过多智能体协调实现成本效益的AI辅导，在保持97.1%优质教学质量的同时降低71.6%服务成本，缩小不同访问层级间的AI教育差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生成式AI导师创造了新的教育不平等：付费用户获得更高质量的教学服务，而免费用户则受到限制。急需一种既能控制成本又能维持教学质量的公平导向方案。

Method: 提出FairTutor公平感知模型路由框架，包含查询分析、教学规划、低成本模型生成、评估器引导的批判修正、选择性升级到高级AI模型等模块；引入AIED优势差距度量指标和TutorAccessEval基准数据集。

Result: FairTutor达到高级教学质量的97.1%（基于地板调整的李克特量表），服务成本降低71.6%；敏感性分析显示存在可调的成本-质量帕累托前沿。

Conclusion: FairTutor能够根据多样化学生群体的需求进行定制化调整，在成本与质量之间实现灵活平衡，有效缓解AI教育中的访问层级不平等问题。

Abstract: Generative AI tutors provide real-time, personalized learning support, but also create a new education inequity: students with access to premium AI services may receive clearer explanations, more personalized guidance, and better scaffolding than students limited to free or low-cost services. To address this challenge, we propose FairTutor, an equity-aware model-routing framework that achieves cost-effective AI tutoring via pedagogically motivated multi-agent orchestration. FairTutor combines query analysis, pedagogical planning, low-cost model generation, evaluator-guided critique and revision, and selective escalation to premium AI models. We introduce access-tier AI Education (AIED) Advantage Gap to measure the quality difference between premium-access and budget-constrained tutoring, and TutorAccessEval, a benchmark spanning math, reading, writing, science, and language learning. Empirical evaluations show that FairTutor achieves 97.1% of premium pedagogical quality (in floor-adjusted Likert scale) while reducing serving cost by 71.6%. Sensitivity analysis reveals a tunable cost--quality Pareto frontier, enabling FairTutor to be tailored to the needs of diverse student populations.

</details>


### [109] [Escape from Delusional Echo Trap: Symmetry Breaking, Stochastic Dynamics and Mathematical Mitigation Strategies for Algorithmic Sycophancy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20718)
*Sayantari Ghosh,Saumik Bhattacharya,Partha Pratim Chakrabarti*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个基于动力系统和随机微分方程的数学框架，用于追踪用户在与具有隐性谄媚特质的AI聊天机器人交互时的认知轨迹。研究发现，谄媚反馈超过临界阈值会导致认知势能景观发生结构相变，形成深度吸引子盆地，使用户陷入自我强化的妄想性信念；而足够强的真实外部信息可以克服内部反馈屏障，实现认知反转并恢复客观信念状态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI谄媚现象研究多停留在经验观察层面，缺乏对用户认知轨迹演化的严格数学建模。本文旨在建立一个定量框架，解释谄媚反馈如何系统性增强用户原有偏见并导致不可逆的认知固化，同时探索如何通过外部信息干预实现认知恢复。

Method: 将用户信念演化建模为连续对数几率状态变量，构建随机微分方程，并将其耦合到多谷势能景观中进行分析。通过动力系统的相变理论考察临界阈值下的结构转变，并分析外部信息作为扰动项对系统的修正作用。

Result: 1) 谄媚反馈超过临界阈值时，认知势能景观发生结构相变，初始偏差被显著加深；2) 系统形成深度、高弹性的吸引子盆地，导致信念固化；3) 足够强且真实的外部信息可以克服内部反馈屏障，诱导认知反转，恢复客观信念状态。

Conclusion: 该框架为理解AI谄媚导致的认知螺旋提供严格的数学基础，揭示了信念固化的相变机制，并指出了通过强外部证据进行认知干预的可行性路径。

Abstract: We propose a rigorous and systematic mathematical framework for tracking the cognitive trajectories of a user, in the context of algorithmic sycophancy and AI-driven delusional spiraling. Using tools from dynamical systems theory and stochastic differential equations, we explore how individuals perceive, interpret, and update their beliefs as they interact with AI chatbots that possess hidden traits of sycophancy. We treat the evolving conviction as a continuous log-odds state variable, coupled into a stochastic differential equation, navigating a multi-valley potential energy landscape. Our analysis reveals several critical observations governing the stability and rigidity of belief dynamics. We demonstrate that the baseline prior perception of the individual is systematically enhanced by sycophantic feedback beyond a critical threshold. Here, the perceptual potential landscape undergoes a structural phase transition that severely deepens any incremental initial tilt present in the baseline state, transforming the landscape and giving rise to deep, highly resilient attractor basins that trap the individual in unshakeable, self-reinforcing, delusional convictions. Finally, we demonstrate that genuine external information can successfully challenge these rigid states. If this incoming evidence is strong and authentic enough to overcome the internal feedback barrier, it can correct the structural asymmetry caused by sycophancy, inducing a perception reversal that successfully restores the objective belief state.

</details>


### [110] [Process-Reward Tactic Evolution for Long-Horizon Bioinformatics Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20839)
*Lingzhi Yang,Yubo Fan,Song Wu,Gilchan Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Process-Reward Tactic Evolution框架，通过Galaxy工作流环境训练LLM智能体执行长周期生物信息学任务，利用过程验证器监督智能体在工作流构建、软件交互、执行监控和生物学正确性等方面的表现，并将成功/失败轨迹蒸馏为可复用策略库，显著提升了长周期工作流完成率、生物学正确性和执行效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体虽能编写代码和调用工具，但生物信息学工作流需要长时间跨度的交互、工作流软件操作、类型化数据对象管理、数据溯源以及生物学验证，现有方法难以满足这些复杂需求。

Method: 提出Process-Reward Tactic Evolution框架：1) 在Galaxy环境中构建Agent Gym，通过课程化组织任务进行训练；2) 使用过程验证器对工作流构建、软件交互、执行和生物学正确性进行评分；3) 将成功和失败轨迹蒸馏为可复用策略库；4) 推理时智能体利用该策略库在隔离环境中执行未见过的生物信息学工作流任务。

Result: 在BioWorkflow Bench和BioAgent Bench任务上，相比于无记忆和反思式基线方法，该框架在长周期生物信息学工作流完成率、生物学正确性和执行效率方面均表现更优。

Conclusion: 过程监督下的策略积累能够有效提升LLM智能体在复杂生物信息学工作流中的长周期任务完成能力，证明了将成功/失败轨迹蒸馏为可复用策略库是一种有效的训练范式。

Abstract: LLM agents can write code and call tools, but reliable bioinformatics work requires long-horizon interaction with workflow software, typed data objects, provenance, and biological checks. We study this setting through Galaxy workflow execution. The agent must explore task data, construct or adapt an executable workflow DAG, bind inputs and dataset collections, monitor execution, debug failures, and validate biological outputs. We propose Process-Reward Tactic Evolution, a Galaxy-based training framework that turns verified workflow rollouts into reusable \tactics. During training, agents practice on curriculum-organized Galaxy tasks in Agent Gym; process verifiers score workflow construction, software interaction, execution, and biological correctness; successful and failed traces are distilled into a tactic library. At inference, the trained executor, Process-Reward Tactic Evolution, uses this library to execute held-out peer reviewed Galaxy workflow converted BioWorkflow Bench and BioAgent Bench tasks in isolated environments. The paper evaluates whether process-supervised tactic accumulation improves long-horizon bioinformatics workflow completion, biological correctness, and execution efficiency over no-memory and reflection-style baselines.

</details>


### [111] [When Do Intrinsic Rewards Work for Code Reasoning? A Comprehensive Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20881)
*Xiaolong Jin,Xuandong Zhao,Wenbo Guo,Xiangyu Zhang,Dawn Song*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了基于内在奖励（如置信度分数、多数投票）的强化学习方法在代码生成任务中的表现，发现这类方法虽然能带来早期收益，但最终会导致模型输出缩短、推理能力丧失，且无法作为RLVR训练的预训练手段提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR方法依赖昂贵的真实标注监督，尤其在代码生成任务中难以获取。虽然内在奖励方法在数学推理上取得成功，但代码生成具有结构复杂、语义等价解存在语法差异、需执行验证等独特挑战，其有效性尚未被探索。

Method: 在LiveCodeBench上系统评估基于置信度的RLIF（Reinforcement Learning from Internal Feedback）方法，在不同训练场景和超参数设置下进行实验，分析样本量、温度等参数的影响。

Result: 内在奖励方法早期表现提升但最终必然崩溃（输出缩短、推理能力丧失），崩溃速度受样本量和温度影响；用于初始化RLVR训练时与从头训练相比无显著优势。

Conclusion: 揭示了内在奖励方法在代码生成任务中的潜力与局限性，提供了使用内在奖励训练代码推理模型的可操作建议，为未来代码模型和智能体研究指明方向。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning with verifiable rewards (RLVR) has driven substantial progress in large language model reasoning, but relies on ground-truth supervision that is costly or infeasible, especially in coding tasks. Recent work addresses this by deriving rewards from a model's own signals, such as majority voting or confidence-based scores, achieving notable success on mathematical reasoning benchmarks. However, code generation poses distinct challenges: programs are structurally complex, semantically equivalent solutions may differ syntactically, and verification typically requires execution. Whether these intrinsic reward methods transfer effectively to code remains unexplored. In this work, we present a systematic empirical study of intrinsic reward methods for code generation. We conduct extensive experiments on LiveCodeBench, systematically evaluating representative certainty-based Reinforcement Learning from Internal Feedback (RLIF) approaches under different training scenarios and hyperparameter settings. Our experiments reveal that certainty-based methods yield early gains but inevitably collapse: models progressively shorten outputs and lose reasoning capability, with collapse speed sensitive to sample size and temperature. When used to initialize RLVR training, RLIF pre-training offers no significant improvement over training from scratch. We also provide actionable recommendations for using intrinsic rewards for training code reasoning models. Our study shows both the promise and limitations of intrinsic reward methods for code, informing future work on code models and agents.

</details>


### [112] [Neurosymbolic Clinical Trial Matching via LLM-Driven Abduction and Logical Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20895)
*Baiyang Qu,Leonardo Ranaldi,Xi Wang,Marco Valentino*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结合大语言模型与逻辑验证的溯因神经符号混合框架αNeSy-CTM，用于自动化临床试验匹配，相比纯LLM基线实现了高达30%的相对性能提升，并展示了与CoT推理的互补性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在临床试验匹配中虽具潜力，但难以处理复杂入组标准所需的确定性验证；纯符号方法虽形式严谨，但面对不完整病历和噪声临床证据时表现不佳。为弥合这一差距，需要探索混合方法。

Method: 提出αNeSy-CTM溯因神经符号框架，利用LLM的语言和世界知识，结合逻辑验证进行溯因推理，以支持在含噪声和欠明确的临床文本上进行推理。

Result: αNeSy-CTM大幅优于纯LLM基线，相对零样本基线提升最高30%；相比非溯因的神经符号设置，在准确率、特异性和鲁棒性上均有提升；且与CoT推理高度互补。

Conclusion: 神经符号方法对自动化CTM具有显著价值，为开发下一代可审计、由LLM驱动的临床应用程序提供了可行路径。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) offer a promising path to automate Clinical Trial Matching (CTM), but still struggle with the deterministic verification required for complex eligibility criteria. Conversely, purely symbolic methods provide formal rigour but break down when faced with incomplete patient records and noisy clinical evidence. To bridge this gap, we investigate a hybrid framework for CTM combining LLMs with logical verification. In particular, we introduce an abductive neurosymbolic CTM framework (αNeSy-CTM), which leverages the linguistic and world knowledge in LLMs to support reasoning over noisy and underspecified clinical text. Extensive evaluation demonstrates that αNeSy-CTM substantially outperforms standalone LLM baselines, achieving up to 30% relative improvement over zero-shot baselines. In addition, our analyses confirm the impact of abductive reasoning on CTM, with αNeSy-CTM exhibiting improved accuracy, specificity, and robustness over a non-abductive neurosymbolic setting. Furthermore, αNeSy-CTM and Chain-of-Thought (CoT) reasoning prove highly complementary, highlighting the potential for a hybrid routing policy. Ultimately, this paper demonstrates the impact of neurosymbolic methods for automating CTM, providing a path toward the next generation of auditable, LLM-driven clinical applications.

</details>


### [113] [AutoACSL: Synthesizing ACSL Specifications by Integrating LLMs with CPG-Based Static Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20969)
*Han Zhou,Yu Luo,Dianxiang Xu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AutoACSL 是一个结合大语言模型与代码属性图静态分析的框架，用于自动化生成 C 程序的 ACSL 形式化规范。在 604 个程序上，与 Gemini-3 配合达到 98% 的规范生成成功率和 96% 的完整证明率，相比纯代码基线提升 24.7%-51.7%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为 C 程序生成形式化规范需要大量人工、专业知识和语义精度，现有 LLM 方法缺乏语义深度且产生不可验证或不完整的契约。

Method: 集成 LLM 提示与从代码属性图提取的语义特征，通过静态分析提取算术运算、循环递归结构、返回值传播等关键语义元素编码到结构化提示中；采用反馈驱动合成循环，用 Frama-C/WP 验证候选规范并迭代精化。

Result: 在 604 个程序上，与 Gemini-3 配合达 98% 规范生成成功率、96% 完整证明率；在四种 LLM 上相比纯代码基线完整证明率提升 24.7%-51.7%。

Conclusion: 将大语言模型与基于 CPG 的静态分析相结合能显著提升自动化 ACSL 规范合成的生成鲁棒性和验证有效性。

Abstract: Generating formal specifications for C programs remains a challenge in formal verification due to the manual effort, expertise, and semantic precision required. While recent advancements in large language models (LLMs) offer promise in automating specification synthesis, current approaches often lack semantic depth and produce unverifiable or incomplete contracts. To address these limitations, we introduce AutoACSL, a novel framework that integrates LLM prompting with semantic features extracted from Code Property Graphs (CPGs). AutoACSL performs static analyses to extract key semantic elements, including arithmetic operations, loop and recursion structures, and return value propagation, which are encoded into structured prompts. These prompts enable the LLM not only to generate normal behavioral specifications but also to include constraints that prevent inputs leading to runtime errors. AutoACSL employs a feedback-driven synthesis loop, where candidate specifications are verified using Frama-C/WP and refined iteratively until verification succeeds or a termination condition is met. Evaluated on 604 programs drawn from diverse datasets, AutoACSL achieves a 98% specification generation success ratio and a 96% full proof ratio when paired with Gemini-3. Compared to a code-only baseline, AutoACSL improves the full proof ratio by 24.7% to 51.7% across four LLMs (GPT-o4 Mini, GPT-5.2, Grok-4.1, and Gemini-3), demonstrating that integrating large language models with CPG-based static analysis substantially enhances both generation robustness and verification effectiveness for automated ACSL specification synthesis.

</details>


### [114] [How Should Agents Read Demonstrations? Hierarchical Structure Beats Flat Action Logs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.20978)
*Honjar Xing,Jefferson Lin,Henry Lieberman*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究在编程式演示（PbD）中，将录制的平铺动作日志组织为带标签的层级子目标，能显著提升LLM Agent在模糊描述任务中的执行成功率（从76.7%提升至90.7%），而平铺格式效果有限。消融实验表明，子目标分组是核心驱动因素，前置/后置条件及参数注释无额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索PbD录制的平铺动作日志在传递给LLM Agent前的最佳组织方式，以提升任务执行质量，使非程序员也能通过演示方式高效为Agent编排程序性知识。

Method: 在85个网页自动化任务上，对比零样本基线与四种共享相同动作序列但结构不同的演示格式。将任务分为43个模糊描述任务和42个精确描述任务，使用配对置换检验评估效果，并进行消融实验。

Result: 层级子目标分组在模糊描述任务中将通过率从76.7%提升至90.7%（p=0.034，胜率6:0），平铺格式改善较小且不显著；精确描述任务中所有格式均无提升；消融实验确认仅子目标分组有效。

Conclusion: 为PbD流水线及任何向LLM Agent提供程序性上下文的系统提供了具体设计建议：应将动作序列分割为带命名的子目标组，而非呈现平铺步骤列表。这种层级优势仅在描述模糊、程序细节不明确时显现。

Abstract: Programming by Demonstration (PbD) offers a human-centered way to author procedural knowledge for LLM agents: users communicate what they want by showing rather than by writing prompts or code, making agent authoring accessible to non-programmers. The natural output of a PbD recording is a flat action log, but how this log is organized before being passed to the agent is an open design question with significant consequences for plan quality. We propose grouping recorded actions into labeled, hierarchical subgoals and evaluate the effect of this organizational structure in a controlled experiment. Across 85 web automation tasks, we compare a zero-shot baseline against four demonstration formats that share identical action sequences but differ in structure. On 43 natural-language tasks with vague descriptions, hierarchically grouped demonstrations improve pass rates from 76.7\% to 90.7\% (paired permutation test $p{=}0.034$; win-loss 6:0), while flat demonstrations show a smaller, non-significant improvement. On 42 tasks with precise descriptions, no format provides any benefit, confirming that the hierarchical advantage arises specifically when descriptions leave procedural details ambiguous. Ablation shows that subgoal grouping alone drives the effect: preconditions, postconditions, and parameter annotations add no measurable benefit. These results offer a concrete design recommendation for PbD pipelines and, more broadly, for any system that feeds procedural context to an LLM agent: segment action sequences into named subgoal groups rather than presenting flat step lists.

</details>


### [115] [Agentic Time Machine as an Infrastructure for Future-Event Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21013)
*Jingyi Chai,Bingyang Zheng,Xiangrui Liu,Hao Lu,Zihang Zhou,Tianchen Wang,Kemeng Zhang,Siheng Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出Agentic Time Machine (TM)基础设施，通过过滤未来内容重建历史网络状态，用于快速评估预测型LLM智能体。同时提出规划-求解-聚合多智能体框架，在FutureX和Polymarket等基准上取得SOTA性能，并验证了离线TM评分与在线FutureX评分的强相关性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM预测评估存在效率与环境保真度的权衡：在线评估反馈周期慢，而离线回放评估依赖静态冻结数据库，缺乏实际部署的环境真实性。作者希望构建一个既能快速反馈又能保持环境真实性的评估基础设施。

Method: 1) Agentic Time Machine (TM)：通过过滤超出时间戳的内容，近似重建任意历史时刻的网络状态。2) 规划-求解-聚合多智能体框架：将问题分解为多个分析角度，并行收集证据，最后合并成单一预测结果。

Result: 离线TM评分与在线FutureX评分强相关；在FutureX-Past和Polymarket评估中，所提框架超越闭书模型、工具增强和自一致性基线；在FutureX实时排行榜上，连续四周平均排名最佳，包括5月第一周第1名，截至6月17日八周总榜第1名。

Conclusion: Agentic Time Machine为预测智能体评估提供了快速且可靠的沙箱环境，所提出的多智能体框架在事件预测任务上达到了当前最优性能。

Abstract: Forecasting future events is a critical challenge for large language model (LLM) agents, spanning domains from elections and monetary policy to financial markets. However, evaluating progress on this task presents a fundamental trade-off between efficiency and environment fidelity. While live evaluation benchmarks suffer from an inherently slow feedback loop, existing retrospective replays typically restrict agents to static, pre-frozen databases that sacrifice the environmental realism of actual deployments. To tackle this issue, we introduce Agentic Time Machine (TM), an infrastructure that approximately reconstructs the web state at any chosen past time by filtering post-cutoff content. Leveraging this evaluation infrastructure, we further propose a planner-solver-aggregator multi-agent framework that breaks each question into diverse analytical angles, gathers evidence in parallel, and combines the results into a single forecast. Experiments show that offline scores under TM correlate strongly with live FutureX scores, validating that TM offers a fast and reliable sandbox for forecasting-agent evaluation. On FutureX-Past and Polymarket evaluated under TM, our framework achieves the highest score among strong closed-book, tool-augmented, and self-consistency baselines. On the official FutureX live leaderboard, our system achieves the best average rank over four consecutive weeks, including 1st place in May Week 1. As of June 17, it also ranks 1st on FutureX's official eight-week overall leaderboard.

</details>


### [116] [SVGym (SciVerseGym): An Environment for Reinforcement Learning and Bayesian Optimization in Crystal Discovery](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.22425)
*Bin Cao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: SciVerseGym是一个基于Gymnasium的晶体发现强化学习环境，将晶体设计建模为马尔可夫决策过程，支持元素替换、晶格扰动、原子位移、空位创建和原子插入等局部与全局操作，可与机器学习势函数或ASE计算器结合，为强化学习、贝叶斯优化、进化搜索和语言智能体工作流提供标准化测试平台。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的闭环晶体搜索方法分散在各自定制的流程中（编辑、弛豫、评分、约束、记账），缺乏统一、可复现、可扩展的标准化环境，阻碍了强化学习、贝叶斯优化等算法在晶体发现中的系统研究和比较。

Method: 将晶体设计建模为马尔可夫决策过程（MDP），构建Gymnasium兼容环境。智能体观测原子结构、执行化学上有意义的编辑操作，并从可配置评估器接收反馈。支持多种动作类型、可配置化学空间、结构池、多种观测方式、自定义奖励、弛豫选项及稳定性/声子诊断。每一步执行编辑后使用机器学习势函数或ASE计算器评估候选结构，返回标准(obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info)元组。

Result: 提出了SciVerseGym这一开放、可复现、可扩展的晶体发现测试平台，支持强化学习、贝叶斯优化、进化搜索和语言智能体工作流在闭环晶体发现中的应用。

Conclusion: 通过将智能体逻辑与材料基础设施解耦，为晶体发现领域的各类优化算法提供了标准化benchmark环境，有助于推动数据驱动的材料自动发现研究。

Abstract: Machine-learned interatomic potentials now enable efficient atomistic evaluation for interactive materials discovery, yet closed-loop crystal search methods remain fragmented across bespoke pipelines for editing, relaxation, scoring, constraints, and bookkeeping. We introduce SciVerseGym, a Gymnasium-compatible environment for sequential crystal discovery that frames crystal design as a Markov decision process. Agents observe an atomistic structure, apply chemically meaningful edits, and receive feedback from a configurable evaluator. SciVerseGym supports local and global actions, including elemental substitution, lattice perturbation, atomic displacement, vacancy creation, and atom insertion, along with configurable chemical spaces, structure pools, atomistic and graph-based observations, custom rewards, optional relaxation, and stability or phonon-related diagnostics. Each step applies an edit, evaluates the candidate using a machine-learned interatomic potential or any ASE-compatible calculator, and returns the standard (obs, reward, terminated, truncated, info) tuple. By decoupling agent logic from materials infrastructure, SciVerseGym provides an open, reproducible, and extensible testbed for reinforcement learning, Bayesian optimization, evolutionary search, and language-agent workflows in closed-loop crystal discovery. Code is available at: https://github.com/Bin-Cao/SciVerseGym.

</details>


### [117] [Negative Knowledge as Failure-aware Shared Memory for AutoResearch](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21024)
*Hanchun Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种负知识记忆层：由策展代理将失败的实验尝试转化为结构化记录存入共享库，下游研究代理在提出新实验前明确采纳或拒绝这些记录。在ScienceAgentBench同任务重试和两个非线性数学物理PDE问题跨任务科研中，负知识层以更少token超越AutoResearch基线，且能解决所有基线均失败的任务，并可跨不同PDE问题迁移增强科研能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前AI辅助科研系统会产生大量失败的尝试，但这些失败很少成为持久、可共享的知识资产，导致重复犯错和资源浪费。

Method: 构建负知识记忆层：(1) 策展代理将每次失败的尝试转化为有边界、有类型标记的记录存入共享库；(2) 下游研究代理在提出下一个实验前，明确采纳或拒绝这些记录。

Result: 在ScienceAgentBench同任务重试和两个非线性数学物理PDE系统研究中，负知识层优于AutoResearch基线且使用更少token；能解决所有基线均失败的新任务；负知识库可跨问题迁移增强科研能力。

Conclusion: 结构化负知识应被视作一种独立的知识资产，与正向发现并列，作为集体科研记忆的基础设施，而不仅仅是记忆压缩或调试辅助工具。

Abstract: AI-assisted research systems generate many failed attempts, but those failures rarely become a durable, shared knowledge asset. We propose a negative knowledge memory layer: a curator agent converts each failed attempt into a bounded, typed record in a shared bank, and a downstream research agent explicitly adopts or rejects those records before proposing its next experiment. We evaluate this layer in two settings: same-task retry on ScienceAgentBench and cross-task scientific research on two nonlinear math-physics PDE problems. The negative knowledge layer outperforms vanilla AutoResearch baselines while using fewer tokens; agents with the negative knowledge bank solve new tasks that all baselines fail to solve in PDE systems research. We also show that the previous negative knowledge bank can transfer and enhance AutoResearch on different PDE problems. These results suggest that structured negative knowledge is a knowledge asset that should be explicitly maintained in broader AI-engaged scientific research beyond a memory-compression or debugging aid, alongside positive findings, as a collective infrastructure for scientific memory. Code is available at https://github.com/hch-wang/Negative_Knowledge.

</details>


### [118] [Coherence Under Commitment: Probing Generalization and Vacuous Memorization in LLM Logical Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21083)
*Noor Islam S. Mohammad,Mahmudul Hasan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出 Coherence Under Commitment (CUC) 评估范式，通过联合衡量一致性与决断力，揭示LLM在逻辑推理中可通过系统性弃权实现虚假的连贯性，实验表明仅评估一致性会错误地将弃权模型排在第一，而CUC暴露了这种空洞性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注一致性（negation consistency），导致模型可通过系统性弃权（既不支持蕴含也不支持反驳）空洞地满足一致性，实际提供零效用。需要一种能同时衡量一致性与决断力的评估方法。

Method: 提出CUC双查询评估范式：(1) 承诺得分 c(φ)=p(φ)+p(¬φ)，量化分配决定性结果的概率质量；(2) 确定性引导协议，通过归一化YES/NO对数概率消除采样方差；(3) 三路决策框架（True/False/Uncertain），将连贯性-承诺权衡操作化为指标。

Result: 在4个开源LLM（1B-3B）和204个FOLIO样本上，Qwen2.5-3B近乎零矛盾（期望矛盾得分0.025）但覆盖率仅7.4%；TinyLlama-1.1B覆盖率79.4%但每个样本均出现矛盾。一致性评估会将弃权模型排第一，CUC揭示其空洞性。该结论在LogiQA v2上泛化（ρ=0.97）。

Conclusion: 评估必须同时报告一致性与非空洞承诺，并发布了标准化评估工具包。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) deployed for logical reasoning in knowledge-intensive domains exhibit a subtle but critical failure: coherence can be vacuously achieved through systematic abstention. A model that withholds commitment to either entailment or refutation satisfies negation consistency while providing no utility. We introduce Coherence Under Commitment (CUC), a dual-query evaluation paradigm that jointly measures consistency and decisiveness. CUC contributes three innovations: (1) a commitment score $c(\varphi) = p(\varphi) + p(\lnot\varphi)$ quantifying probability mass allocated to decisive outcomes; (2) a \textbf{deterministic elicitation protocol} via normalized YES/NO log probabilities, eliminating sampling variance; and (3) a 3-way decision framework (True/False/Uncertain) operationalizing the coherence-commitment trade-off into metrics. Experiments on four open-weight LLMs (1B-3B) across 204 FOLIO examples expose a sharp frontier. Qwen2.5-3B achieves near-zero contradiction ($\mathbb{E}[v_{\mathrm{neg}}]{=}0.025$) but only $7.4\%$ coverage, while TinyLlama-1.1B reaches $79.4\%$ coverage with violations on every example. Coherence-only evaluation would rank the abstaining model first; CUC exposes this as vacuous, and the frontier generalizes to LogiQA~v2 ($ρ{=}0.97$). We argue that evaluation must report both coherence and non-vacuous commitment and release a toolkit for standardized assessment.

</details>


### [119] [Repeated post-training is not Self-improving: Diagnosing Scientific Amnesia in Continual DPO Pipelines](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21089)
*Jianzhe Lin,Fei Wang,Xiaolin Li,Rajeshkumar Golani,Jubin Chheda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出并定义了工业LLM团队在持续DPO训练中遇到的一种新失败模式——"科学遗忘"（scientific amnesia），即模型虽能保留已学行为，但无法积累关于如何训练下一轮次的可复用方法论知识。作者构建了诊断工具集、基于程序的流水线、30轮次HumanEval基准，并对五种策略进行了比较研究。核心发现是不同策略效果高度依赖于链式训练设置、评估器设计和种子覆盖范围，而非存在一种通用解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业LLM团队在反复对基座模型进行序列化偏好数据DPO训练时，发现主要失败模式并非经典的灾难性遗忘（catastrophic forgetting），而是一种新的现象：流水线虽能保留已学行为，但无法积累关于如何训练下一轮次的可复用方法论知识。作者将这一实践经验转化为可测的工业问题，旨在系统性地诊断和研究该现象。

Method: (i) 设计了一套科学遗忘诊断套件；(ii) 构建了基于程序的流水线，可在Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct模型上链式执行FSDP分片的DPO检查点；(iii) 创建了包含30轮次HumanEval子领域的基准测试；(iv) 对五种策略（随机记忆、基于规则的调度、仅检索记忆、热启动贝叶斯优化、MSCL元科学记忆与推理器）进行了比较诊断研究，在单种子5条件×3步真实LLM链、异质链和小规模多种子同质链等不同设置下进行了实验。

Result: 在单种子5条件×3步真实LLM链中，5种候选策略中有4种（包括MSCL）在步骤级峰值pass@1指标上出现退化，只有保守的基于规则调度有所改善。后续实验进一步限定该结论：异质链中，MSCL是唯一完成且有所改善的候选策略；小规模多种子同质链中，仅检索记忆策略具有最佳平均Delta，但所有候选策略之间的成对差异在统计上不显著。

Conclusion: 本文的核心贡献是诊断性的，而非宣称MSCL解决了该问题。科学遗忘在生产级别的持续DPO流水线中是可观测的真实现象，且关于干预措施的有效性结论高度依赖于链式训练设置、评估器设计和种子覆盖范围。这一发现呼吁工业界在持续训练场景下更加审慎地评估策略选择和实验设计。

Abstract: Industrial LLM teams often ship behavior updates by repeatedly DPO-training a base model on sequences of related preference-data campaigns. The dominant failure mode in this regime is not always classical catastrophic forgetting: a pipeline may preserve previously learned behaviors while still failing to accumulate reusable methodological knowledge about how to train the next campaign. We call this failure mode scientific amnesia. This paper turns that practitioner intuition into a measurable industrial problem. We contribute: (i) a diagnostic suite for amnesia, (ii) a Program-based pipeline that chains FSDP-sharded DPO checkpoints across Qwen2.5-7B-Instruct runs, (iii) a 30-campaign HumanEval subdomain benchmark, and (iv) a comparative diagnostic study of five strategy proposers: random memory, rule-based scheduling, retrieval-only memory, warm-start Bayesian optimization, and MSCL, a meta-scientific memory and reasoner candidate. Across a single-seed 5-condition * 3-step real-LM chain, 4 of 5 candidates degrade in step-level peak pass@1, including MSCL; only the deliberately conservative rule-based schedule improves. Follow-up pilots qualify rather than overturn this finding: in a heterogeneous chain, MSCL is the only completed candidate that improves, whereas in a small multi-seed homogeneous sweep, retrieval-only has the best mean Delta and no pairwise candidate gap is statistically distinguishable. The contribution is therefore diagnostic, not a claim that MSCL solves the problem: scientific amnesia is observable in a production-like continual-DPO pipeline, and conclusions about interventions depend sharply on chain regime, evaluator design, and seed coverage.

</details>


### [120] [Self-Improvement Can Self-Regress: The Rise-and-Collapse Failure Mode of LLM Self-Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21090)
*Jianzhe Lin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 在代码强化学习训练中，模型在优化指标上会先快速提升后崩溃（rise-then-collapse），这不是灾难性遗忘而是同分布策略过度优化。论文比较了三种控制层级：跨训练营记忆机制CARE、早停规则ES、以及组相对奖励归一化GRPO，发现最优策略取决于模型规模和数据环境。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: REINFORCE后训练中模型会在同一训练过程中先提升后崩溃，需要探究这种现象的本质并寻找有效的控制机制。

Method: 在Qwen-2.5-3B/7B上使用二进制CodeGrader奖励的竞争编程任务，进行10个连续20步训练营的多种子实验。比较三种控制层级：CARE（跨训练营记忆机制，含能力后验、迁移门和回归感知信念修正）、ES（训练营内早停规则）、GRPO（组相对奖励归一化）。

Result: CARE v2在Qwen-2.5-3B上将端到链pass@1从4.9%提升至9.5%；在7B上CARE与基线持平（13.8% vs 11.8%），而ES达到22.2%，GRPO达到20.7%。GRPO提高了下限但未消除崩溃，其7B增益主要来自跨训练营传递，训练营内峰值到尾声差距仍约17个百分点。

Conclusion: 最优控制策略存在规模和数据依赖性：小模型上CARE有效，大模型上ES和GRPO表现更优。GRPO能提高下限但无法消除崩溃现象，且该现象在Gemma-3-4B上同样存在，具有跨模型通用性。

Abstract: Self-improvement can self-regress. In REINFORCE post-training for code, a model can quickly improve on its optimized metric and then collapse within the same training campaign. We study this in a controlled multi-seed testbed using Qwen-2.5-3B and Qwen-2.5-7B, trained on competitive-programming tasks with binary CodeGrader reward across 10 sequential 20-step campaigns. Across campaigns, pass@1 shows a robust rise-then-collapse pattern: it peaks within tens of gradient steps and then falls back, sometimes to near zero. This is not cross-task catastrophic forgetting, but within-task policy over-optimization on a fixed distribution; KL- and EWC-style constraints do not prevent it.
  We ask where the control loop should sit. We compare three levels: CARE, a between-campaign memory mechanism with a capability posterior, transfer gate, and regression-aware belief revision; ES, a within-campaign early-stop rule that rolls forward the peak checkpoint and sets the next budget to peak_step+3; and GRPO, which changes the RL update using group-relative reward normalization.
  The answer is regime-dependent. On Qwen-2.5-3B, where naive REINFORCE is fragile, CARE v2 nearly doubles end-of-chain pass@1 from 4.9% to 9.5%, with paired bootstrap 95% CI [+0.4,+8.9] and gains in 4/5 seeds. On Qwen-2.5-7B, CARE reaches parity with naive REINFORCE, 13.8% vs. 11.8%, while ES reaches 22.2% [14.1,28.0]. Out-of-the-box GRPO reaches 20.7% [15.7,25.1], nearly matching REINFORCE+ES.
  GRPO raises the floor but does not remove the cliff. Its 7B gain mainly comes from better between-campaign carryover, while the within-campaign peak-to-end gap remains about 17 points under both REINFORCE and GRPO. GRPO+ES gives mixed evidence: 2/3 seeds improve, but one final cliff lowers the mean to 17.0% [0.0,28.1]. A Gemma-3-4B pilot shows the same signature, suggesting the phenomenon is not limited to Qwen.

</details>


### [121] [Learning Burst-Aware Early Warning Models for Capacity Stress under AI Workload Surges in Hyperscale Data Centers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21130)
*Zihan Yu,Xianling Zeng,Zhiming Xue,Yalun Qi,Sichen Zhao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种面向部署的、爆发感知的早期预警框架，用于在AI工作负载激增下主动预测数据中心容量压力。该框架将问题建模为多变量遥测窗口上的高召回率预测任务，采用轻量级XGBoost模型捕捉高度不平衡环境中的非线性交互。在AI工作负载激增注入的评估下，模型在部署导向的阈值选择下实现了0.914的召回率，有效检测大部分压力期，并支持工作负载节流和资源扩展等主动干预。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型训练和推理等大规模AI工作负载的快速增长从根本上改变了超大规模数据中心的运营动态。与传统的云工作负载不同，AI驱动的任务表现出突发性、高强度和快速变化的资源需求，常导致突发的容量压力，而基于反应式阈值的方法无法有效应对这种压力。

Method: 将问题建模为多变量遥测窗口上的高召回率预测任务，集成工作负载强度、时间变化和系统压力信号，使用轻量级XGBoost模型处理高度不平衡的环境，并提出AI工作负载激增注入方法来模拟大规模AI系统中的突发需求模式。

Result: XGBoost模型实现了ROC AUC为0.697，AP为0.670，显著优于基线方法。在部署导向的阈值选择下，框架实现了0.914的召回率，能够以可接受的误报成本检测大部分压力易发期。

Conclusion: 所提框架可集成到运营控制回路中，支持工作负载节流和资源扩展等主动操作，在高召回率、基于学习的早期预警系统方面具有实际价值，有助于在AI驱动工作负载时代实现弹性自适应的数据中心运营。

Abstract: The rapid growth of large-scale AI workloads, particularly Large Language Model (LLM) training and inference, is fundamentally reshaping the operational dynamics of hyperscale data centers. Unlike traditional cloud workloads, AI-driven jobs exhibit bursty, high-intensity, and rapidly shifting resource demands, often leading to sudden capacity stress that cannot be effectively handled by reactive threshold-based mechanisms. In this paper, we propose a deployment-oriented, burst-aware early warning framework for proactive capacity stress prediction under AI workload surges. We formulate the problem as a high-recall forecasting task over multivariate telemetry windows, with the explicit goal of enabling operational intervention before system degradation occurs. The proposed framework integrates workload intensity, temporal variation, and system pressure signals, and employs a lightweight tree-based learning model to capture nonlinear interactions in highly imbalanced environments. To evaluate the system under realistic conditions, we introduce an AI workload surge injection methodology that simulates burst-driven demand patterns observed in large-scale AI systems. Our XGBoost-based model achieves an ROC AUC of 0.697 and an AP of 0.670, significantly outperforming baseline methods. Under deployment-oriented threshold selection, the framework achieves a Recall of 0.914, enabling the detection of the majority of stress-prone periods with acceptable false-alarm cost. Beyond predictive performance, we show how the proposed framework can be integrated into operational control loops to support proactive actions such as workload throttling and resource scaling. Our results highlight the practical value of high-recall, learning-based early warning systems in enabling resilient and adaptive data center operations in the era of AI-driven workloads.

</details>


### [122] [Trip+: Benchmarking Agents in Personalized Interactive Travel Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21169)
*Junle Chen,Wei Chen,Yehong Xu,Zhengjun Huang,Yuqian Wu,Zhoujin Tian,Kai Wang,Lei Wang,Xiaofang Zhou*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出了Trip+基准，用于评估语言代理在交互式旅行规划中生成和修订分钟级行程的能力，发现现有模型倾向于生成技术上可行但忽略旅行者偏好的疲惫行程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准在评估交互式旅行规划时，往往将可行性、个性化和交互性割裂开，缺乏一个能够整体衡量代理规划能力的测试框架。

Method: 构建Trip+基准，要求代理根据旅行者档案和动态交互生成并修订分钟级行程，使用基于LLM的模拟器评估端到端旅行体验，包括疲劳度等主观指标。

Result: 评估了18个语言模型，发现模型在体验质量上存在一致差距，倾向于生成技术上可行但令人疲惫的行程，与旅行者档案偏好严重偏离。

Conclusion: 现有语言代理在整体性旅行规划中缺乏对旅行者体验的感知，需要改进以更好地平衡可行性与个性化偏好。

Abstract: Interactive travel planning has become a popular use case for language models. Agents are deployed to manage evolving preferences and unexpected disruptions over multiple turns. Such settings require models to make complex, profile-conditioned planning decisions. However, existing benchmarks often evaluate feasibility, personalization, or interaction in relatively isolated settings. We therefore introduce Trip+ to measure the ability of agents to plan travel holistically. In Trip+, given traveler profiles and dynamic interactions, agents must generate and revise minute-level itineraries. End-to-end traveler experiences are evaluated via an LLM-based simulator, enabling the assessment of subjective metrics like fatigue. Our scenarios range from simple request resolutions to complex environment-driven replanning. We evaluate 18 LMs and find a consistent gap in experiential quality. Models favor technically feasible but exhausting itineraries that diverge sharply from profiled traveler preferences.

</details>


### [123] [ARCO: Adaptive Rubric with Co-Evolution for Multi-Step LLM-Based Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21262)
*Zihang Tian,Jingsen Zhang,Rui Li,Xiaohe Bo,Yuanzi Li,Xu Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: ARCO提出了一种自适应评分标准协同进化框架，让同规模模型μ共享骨干网络，同时生成逐步骤评分标准并预测基于评分标准的步骤级奖励，通过轨迹分解约束实现无需步骤标签的信用分配，并使μ与策略π在在线数据上联合更新，实现评分标准与评分函数的参数级协同进化。在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue上，ARCO在所有设置下超越了强基线方法，取得了最佳EM结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的多步LLM智能体强化学习依赖标量奖励，无法解释轨迹好坏；基于评分标准的奖励虽具可解释性，但现有方法在轨迹级别评分且裁判模型固定不变，导致步骤级信用分配问题未解决，且裁判模型本身是静态的。

Method: 提出ARCO框架，采用同规模模型μ共享骨干网络，包含两个头部：生成头（逐步骤生成评分标准）和评分头（基于评分标准预测步骤级奖励）。通过轨迹分解约束将步骤奖励之和与最终结果绑定，实现无需步骤标签的信用分配；同时μ与策略π在在线数据上参数级联合更新，使评分标准内容与评分函数协同进化。

Result: 在HotpotQA、2WikiMultiHopQA和MuSiQue三个数据集上，使用两个开源骨干模型，ARCO在所有设置下均取得了最佳EM结果，超越了强基线的结果、评分标准和过程奖励方法。分析表明其评分标准具有步骤特异性、对设计选择鲁棒，且有助于诊断智能体行为。

Conclusion: ARCO通过参数级评分标准与评分函数的协同进化，有效解决了多步LLM智能体的步骤级信用分配问题，在多个多跳问答数据集上取得最优性能，同时保持了可解释性和诊断能力。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning for multi-step LLM agents often relies on scalar rewards that indicate success but cannot explain why a trajectory is good or bad. Rubric-based rewards improve interpretability through natural-language criteria, but existing methods score at the trajectory level and freeze the scorer behind a closed-source judge, leaving step-level credit assignment unresolved and the judge itself static. We propose ARCO (Adaptive Rubric CO-evolution), a rubric framework in which a same-scale model $μ$ shares a backbone with two heads: a generation head that produces per-step criteria, and a score head that predicts rubric-conditioned step-level rewards. A trajectory decomposition constraint ties the sum of step rewards to the terminal outcome, enabling credit assignment without step-level labels, while $μ$ and the policy $π$ are jointly updated on on-policy data so that the rubric content and the scoring function co-evolve at the parameter level. Across HotpotQA, 2WikiMultiHopQA, and MuSiQue with two open-source backbones, ARCO improves the best EM in every setting over strong outcome-, rubric-, and process-reward baselines, and analyses show that its rubrics are step-specific, robust to design choices, and useful for diagnosing agent behavior. Codes and data are available at https://github.com/zihangtian/ARCO.

</details>


### [124] [Calibration Is Not Control: Why LLM-Agent Oversight Needs Intervention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21399)
*Chubin Zhang,Zhenglin Wan,Xingrui Yu,Jingxuan Wu,Qi Wen,Pengfei Zhou,Wangbo Zhao,Ivor Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文论证LLM代理的运行时监督应从标量风险评分转向行动条件价值估计，提出“干预优势”作为决策对象，并通过前缀分支协议在四个基准上验证了行动条件控制能显著降低控制遗憾。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有监督框架将LLM代理运行时监督简化为标量风险预测（估计失败概率、置信度或不确定性），但本文认为这种框架瞄准了错误的控制目标。关键问题不是代理继续执行时的失败概率，而是可用干预措施能否改善结果。两个具有相同风险估计的轨迹前缀可能需要不同干预，因为一个可恢复而另一个不可恢复。

Method: 本文形式化了标量风险与决策对象之间的错位为“目标误差”（target error），并引入“干预优势”（intervention advantage）作为监督的决策对象——即干预相比继续执行的期望效用增益。为测量这种错位，提出“前缀分支”（prefix branching），一种相同前缀反事实协议，从相同的轨迹状态执行候选动作。在四个基准上比较行动条件控制与标量路由的性能。

Result: 行动条件控制在四个基准上展现了相对于标量路由的情境依赖增益。在标定分解中，重新标定相同标量分数改善了预测指标但未改变控制遗憾，表明标定本身不能修复目标误差。简单的仅前缀行动条件控制器在最强交互情境下大幅降低遗憾（ALFWorld上从0.506降至0.110）。当干预较弱或标量路由已保留干预相关信息时，增益缩小。

Conclusion: LLM代理的监督应从标定风险评分转向行动条件价值估计。

Abstract: Runtime oversight for LLM agents is commonly framed as scalar risk prediction: estimate failure likelihood, confidence, or uncertainty, then intervene once the score crosses a threshold. We argue that this framing targets the wrong object for control. The relevant question is not how likely the agent is to fail if it continues, but whether an available intervention would improve the outcome. Two trajectory prefixes can have the same risk estimate while requiring different actions, because one remains recoverable and the other does not. We formalize this mismatch as target error and identify intervention advantage, the expected utility gain from intervening rather than continuing, as the decision object for oversight. To measure this mismatch, we introduce prefix branching, a same-prefix counterfactual protocol that executes candidate actions from identical trajectory states. Across four benchmarks, action-conditioned control yields regime-dependent gains over scalar routing. In a calibration decomposition, recalibrating the same scalar score improves prediction metrics but leaves control regret unchanged, showing that calibration alone does not repair target error. A simple prefix-only action-conditioned controller substantially reduces regret in the strongest interactive regime, from 0.506 to 0.110 on ALFWorld. Gains shrink when interventions are weak or when scalar routing already preserves intervention-relevant information. These results suggest that LLM-agent oversight should move from calibrated risk scoring toward action-conditioned value estimation.

</details>


### [125] [Don't Blindly Trust It: How Unreliable Feedback Breaks Tool-Using LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21409)
*Chubin Zhang,Zhenglin Wan,Xingrui Yu,Pengfei Zhou,Wangbo Zhao,Jingxuan Wu,Yaxin Zhou,Ivor Tsang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过受控对比实验发现，当反馈不可靠时，工具增强型智能体的性能可能低于无反馈基线（即在HotpotQA上Qwen2.5-7B从44.8 F1降至4.7 F1），表明干净工具带来的增益可能高估工具的真实价值，匹配的无反馈对照是评估工具增强智能体的必要方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估通常假设外部反馈可靠，忽略了反馈不可靠时的反事实问题：智能体在没有任务证据时是否会表现更好？本文系统研究这一关键问题。

Method: 采用匹配的闭环对比实验，固定智能体循环、提示、动作空间和解码方式，仅改变返回的观测类型（忠实/误导/缺失），在问答和事实验证任务上进行评估。

Result: 持续误导性反馈导致价值反转：HotpotQA上Qwen2.5-7B在干净检索下F1为44.8，无反馈时为22.3，但在打乱检索下骤降至4.7。该反转在更强干净检索和局部合理干扰下持续存在，但后期干净证据可修复轨迹时减弱。

Conclusion: 干净工具增益可能高估工具价值，匹配无反馈对照是评估工具增强智能体的必要控制手段；早期轨迹信号可预测多数失败，但简单修复受限于回退策略的可靠性。

Abstract: Tool-augmented agents are typically evaluated by their gains under reliable external feedback. Yet these gains leave open a key counterfactual: when feedback is unreliable, would the agent be better off receiving no task evidence? We study this question with a controlled matched-loop comparison that fixes the agent loop, prompt, action space, and decoding, while varying only the returned observation: faithful, misleading, or absent. Across question answering and fact verification, persistent misleading feedback produces a value inversion: agents that benefit from clean tools can perform worse than the matched no-feedback fallback. On HotpotQA, Qwen2.5-7B reaches 44.8 F1 with clean retrieval and 22.3 F1 with no feedback, but drops to 4.7 F1 under shuffled retrieval. The inversion persists under stronger clean retrieval and locally plausible distractors, but weakens when later clean evidence can repair the trajectory. Early trajectory signals predict many failures, yet simple repairs remain fallback-limited: rejecting bad evidence helps only when the exposed fallback is reliable. These results show that clean-tool gains can overstate tool value, and that matched no-feedback fallback controls are necessary for evaluating tool-augmented agents.

</details>


### [126] [Composing Verifiable Conceptual Models via Building Blocks: Towards Design-Time Verification of Agentic AI Workflows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21565)
*Noe Y. Flandre,Alexander C. Nwala,Philippe J. Giabbanelli*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向Agentic AI系统工作流的设计时验证方法，通过12条结构规则检查可复用构建块的兼容性，并在两个公开数据集上验证了该方法能可靠检测设计缺陷。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前Agentic AI平台侧重于运行时安全，缺乏设计时对工作流进行验证的支持，这类似于在建模与仿真中未验证概念模型构建块之间的一致性。

Method: 将Agentic工作流建模为可复用构建块的组合，通过12条结构规则检查其兼容性；在软件原型中实现规则，并在两个公开数据集（48个含已知缺陷的工作流和168个结构变异但逻辑不变的工作流）上评估。

Result: 验证器能可靠检测出设计缺陷，即使缺陷通过结构变换（如任务拆分）被隐藏，仍能有效识别违规。

Conclusion: 所提方法填补了Agentic工作流设计时验证的空白，未来可与社区构建块仓库结合，以实现安全工作流的组合。

Abstract: Agentic AI systems orchestrate multiple LLM-based agents through workflow architectures that coordinate decisions, tools, and external actions. While current platforms emphasize runtime safeguards, little support exists for verifying workflows during system design. From a Modeling \& Simulation perspective, this gap is analogous to composing conceptual models without verifying whether their building blocks interact coherently. We propose a design-time verification approach that models agentic workflows as compositions of reusable building blocks and checks their compatibility through twelve structural rules. We implemented these rules in a software prototype and evaluated them using two openly released datasets: 48 workflows with known design flaws and 168 variants that preserve workflow logic but alter graph structure. Results show that our verifier reliably detects violations even when flawed designs are obscured through structural transformations such as splitting tasks between agents. Future works could combine our verification with community repositories of building blocks to compose safe agentic workflows.

</details>


### [127] [Counsel: A Meta-Evaluation Dataset for Agentic Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21627)
*Sashank Pisupati,Henry Broomfield,Eujeong Choi,Antonia Calvi,Charlie Wang,Roman Engeler,Max Bartolo,Patrick Lewis*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了Counsel，首个用于智能体任务的元评估公开数据集，包含开源LLM裁判对tau-bench和DA-Code基准的轨迹级批评及其人工元评估注释，用于校准和改进基于LLM的智能体评估器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体系统在复杂多步任务中的评估存在瓶颈，人工标注单条轨迹耗时数小时，难以规模化；当前广泛使用LLM-as-a-judge进行自动化评估，但其批评的准确性常常未经衡量。

Method: 构建Counsel数据集：使用开源LLM裁判对tau-bench（客服智能体）和DA-Code（代码智能体）两个基准生成过程级批评；由人工注释者对每条标记错误标注"准确"、"位置正确但推理差"或"不应标记"，达到0.78的Krippendorff's alpha一致性。

Result: 数据集按轨迹内错误位置和推理质量分层，最强的开源裁判在位置标注上达到约88%的人类一致性，在推理质量上约65%。更强大的裁判模型和更多推理努力均能提升人类一致性。

Conclusion: Counsel数据集采用宽松许可公开，可用于校准、改进或训练LLM裁判，推动基于LLM的智能体评估器的严谨研究和对齐改进。

Abstract: As agentic systems tackle increasingly complex multi-step tasks, evaluating their trajectories presents a major bottleneck - human annotation of a single trajectory on popular agentic benchmarks can take hours, making it difficult to scale evaluations for measuring performance or curating training data. This has driven widespread reliance on automated approaches such as LLM-as-a-judge (LLMJ) to critique agents at the process and outcome-levels at scale, however, the soundness of LLMJ critiques often goes unmeasured. Here, we introduce Counsel, the first public dataset of meta-evaluations for agentic tasks. Counsel consists of process-level critiques from open-weight LLMJs on two agent benchmarks: tau-bench (customer support agents) and DA-Code (coding agents), and human meta-evaluations of these critiques. Human annotators label critiques on each flagged error as "spot on", "correct location but poor reasoning", or "should not have flagged", achieving reliable inter-annotator agreement (Krippendorff's alpha of 0.78). The resulting dataset stratifies LLMJ critiques by human alignment across both error location within a trajectory and reasoning quality, serving as valuable data to calibrate, improve, or train LLMJs for agents. Comparing open-weight judges, we find that more capable judge models and more reasoning effort both enabled improved human agreement, with the strongest judge reaching ~88% agreement on location and ~65% on reasoning. Counsel is generated using open-weight models and is permissively licensed for broad community use, which we hope will enable rigorous study and improved alignment of LLM-based evaluators for agentic systems.

</details>


### [128] [Hallucination as Context Drift: Synchronization Protocols for Multi-Agent LLM Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.21666)
*Carson Rodrigues*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 多智能体LLM系统中的幻觉问题源于智能体间的上下文漂移（知识状态不一致），而非模型能力不足。本文提出上下文分歧分数（CDS）和共享状态验证协议（SSVP），通过周期性交换压缩状态摘要来减少幻觉。实验表明，全广播同步会污染传播错误状态导致幻觉率上升34%，而SSVP在减少58%API调用的情况下显著降低幻觉率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多智能体LLM系统常产生无法用模型缺陷解释的幻觉输出，作者发现根本原因在于并发智能体间的上下文漂移（即内部知识状态分歧），而非模型能力不足。

Method: 提出上下文分歧分数（CDS）量化智能体对间在空间、时间、任务维度上的知识状态差异；提出共享状态验证协议（SSVP），让智能体在联合推理前周期性交换压缩状态摘要并标记高分歧条件。

Result: 在旅行和软件规划两个领域的实验中：全广播同步导致幻觉率比基线上升34%（HR: 0.658 vs 0.492, p=0.0022, d=1.18）；SSVP实现持续降低（HR: 0.463, d=0.30），相比全广播显著更优（p=0.0005, d=1.47）且减少58%API调用。污染效应在软件领域未复现，所有条件HR均低于0.2。

Conclusion: 将幻觉缓解重新定义为分布式系统问题，确立上下文同步作为多智能体LLM设计中的第一类原语。

Abstract: Multi-agent LLM systems routinely produce hallucinated outputs that cannot be explained by model deficiencies alone. A significant class of these failures arises not from model incapacity but from context drift: the divergence of internal knowledge states between concurrent agents. When agents enter a collaborative task with mismatched or stale representations of shared world state, their joint reasoning produces contradictions that manifest as hallucination.
